        &= \int \frac{1}{\sqrt{\tan x} + \sec x} \cdot \frac{2\sin x \sqrt{\tan x} + 1}{2\cos^2 x \sqrt{\tan x}} \, dx \\
        &= \int \frac{1}{u} \, du = \ln|u| + C
        \end{align*}
        代回变量得 
        \[
        I = \ln|\sec x + \sqrt{\tan x}| + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int \frac{16}{(x+6)(x-2)\sqrt{x+2}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $u = \sqrt{x+2}$,则 $x = u^2-2, dx = 2u\,du$。代入得
        \[
        I = \int \frac{16}{(u^2+4)(u^2-4)u} \cdot 2u\,du = \int \frac{32}{(u^2+4)(u^2-4)}\,du
        \]
        应用部分分式分解:
        \[
        \frac{32}{(u^2+4)(u^2-4)} = \frac{4}{u^2-4} - \frac{4}{u^2+4}
        \]
        积分得
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int \left( \frac{1}{u-2} - \frac{1}{u+2} - \frac{4}{u^2+4} \right) \,du \\
        &= \ln\left| \frac{u-2}{u+2} \right| - 2\arctan\left( \frac{u}{2} \right) + C
        \end{align*}
        代回 $u = \sqrt{x+2}$ 得
        \[
        I = \ln\left| \frac{\sqrt{x+2}-2}{\sqrt{x+2}+2} \right| - 2\arctan\left( \frac{\sqrt{x+2}}{2} \right) + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    

    \question 
    \[
    \int \frac{x^4 - 1}{x^2 \sqrt{x^4 + 1}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        应用配凑法
        \[
        I = \int \frac{x^2 - \frac{1}{x^2}}{\sqrt{x^4 + 1}} \, dx = \int \frac{x - \frac{1}{x^3}}{\sqrt{x^2 + \frac{1}{x^2}}} \, dx
        \]
        此时注意到
        \[
        \frac{d}{dx}\left(x^2 + \frac{1}{x^2}\right) = 2x - \frac{2}{x^3} = 2\left(x - \frac{1}{x^3}\right)
        \]
        于是原积分可化为
        \[
        I = \frac{1}{2} \int \frac{d(x^2 + \frac{1}{x^2})}{\sqrt{x^2 + \frac{1}{x^2}}}
        = \sqrt{x^2 + \frac{1}{x^2}} + C
        = \frac{\sqrt{x^4 + 1}}{x} + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 已使用代换 $x^2 + x + 2 = (u-x)^2$,求 
    \[
    \int \frac{1}{x\sqrt{x^2+x+2}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        由已知关系得 
        \[
        x^2 + x + 2 = u^2 - 2ux + x^2 \Rightarrow x = \frac{u^2-2}{2u+1}
        \]
        则
        \[
        dx = \frac{2(u^2+u+2)}{(2u+1)^2} \, du
        \]
        注意到 
        \[
        \sqrt{x^2+x+2} = u-x = u - \frac{u^2-2}{2u+1} = \frac{u^2+u+2}{2u+1}
        \]
        代入积分得
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int \frac{1}{\frac{u^2-2}{2u+1} \cdot \frac{u^2+u+2}{2u+1}} \cdot \frac{2(u^2+u+2)}{(2u+1)^2} \, du \\
        &= \int \frac{2}{u^2-2} \, du \\
        &= \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \ln \left| \frac{u-\sqrt{2}}{u+\sqrt{2}} \right| + C \\
        &= \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \ln \left| \frac{x + \sqrt{x^2+x+2} - \sqrt{2}}{x + \sqrt{x^2+x+2} + \sqrt{2}} \right| + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_{0.2}^{0.5} \frac{\sqrt{x-x^2}}{x^4} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        将被积函数变形:
        \[
        \frac{\sqrt{x-x^2}}{x^4} = \frac{1}{x^3}\sqrt{\frac{1}{x}-1}
        \]
        设 $u = \sqrt{\dfrac{1}{x}-1}$,则 
        \[
        x = \frac{1}{u^2+1} \Rightarrow dx = -\frac{2u}{(u^2+1)^2} \, du
        \]
        于是
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int_{2}^{1} (u^2+1)^3 \cdot u \cdot \frac{-2u}{(u^2+1)^2} \, du \\
        &= \int_{1}^{2} 2u^2(u^2+1) \, du = \int_{1}^{2} (2u^4 + 2u^2) \, du \\
        &= \left[ \frac{2}{5}u^5 + \frac{2}{3}u^3 \right]_{1}^{2} = \frac{256}{15}
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{dx}{x^2(a + bx)^2},\ a,b\in\mathbb{R}
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 \( u = \dfrac{a + bx}{x} \),则
        \[
        x = \frac{a}{u - b}, \quad 
        dx = \frac{-a\,du}{(u - b)^2}, \quad 
        a + bx = x u = \frac{a u}{u - b}
        \]
        代入原式:
        \begin{align*}
        \int \frac{dx}{x^2(a + bx)^2}
        &= \int \frac{-a\,du}{(u - b)^2} \cdot \frac{1}{x^2 (a + bx)^2} \\
        &= \int \frac{-a\,du}{(u - b)^2} \cdot \frac{(u - b)^4}{a^4 u^2} \\
        &= -\frac{1}{a^3} \int \frac{(u - b)^2}{u^2} \, du \\
        &= -\frac{1}{a^3} \int \left( 1 - \frac{2b}{u} + \frac{b^2}{u^2}\right) du \\
        &= -\frac{1}{a^3} \left( u - 2b \ln|u| - \frac{b^2}{u} \right) + C
        \end{align*}
        代回 \( u = \dfrac{a + bx}{x} \)得
        \[
        \int \frac{dx}{x^2(a + bx)^2}
        = -\frac{1}{a^3} \left( \frac{a + bx}{x} - 2b \ln\left| \frac{a + bx}{x} \right| - \frac{b^2 x}{a + bx} \right) + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 使用代换 $\sqrt{5-4x-x^2} = (1-x)u$,其中 $x\neq 1$,求 
    \[
    \int \frac{x}{(5-4x-x^2)^{\frac{3}{2}}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        代换给出
        \[
        5-4x-x^2=(1-x)(x+5) = (1-x)^2u^2 \Rightarrow x = \frac{u^2-5}{u^2+1} \Rightarrow dx = \frac{12u}{(u^2+1)^2} \, du
        \]
        由 $1-x = \dfrac{6}{u^2+1}$ 得,
        \begin{align*}
        I =\int \frac{x}{(5-4x-x^2)^{\frac{3}{2}}} \, dx 
        &= \int \frac{u^2-5}{u^2+1} \cdot \frac{1}{\left(\frac{6u}{u^2+1}\right)^3} \cdot \frac{12u}{(u^2+1)^2} \, du \\
        &= \int \frac{u^2-5}{u^2+1} \cdot \frac{(u^2+1)^3}{216u^3} \cdot \frac{12u}{(u^2+1)^2} \, du \\
        &= \frac{1}{18} \int \frac{u^2-5}{u^2} \, du \\
        &= \frac{1}{18} \left( u + \frac{5}{u} \right) + C
        \end{align*}
        代回 $u = \dfrac{\sqrt{5-4x-x^2}}{1-x}$,整理得 
        \[
        I = \frac{5-2x}{9\sqrt{5-4x-x^2}} + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int \frac{\sqrt{1 + \sqrt[3]{x}}}{\sqrt[3]{x^2}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        注意到 
        \[
        d(\sqrt[3]{x}) = \frac{1}{3\sqrt[3]{x^2}} \, dx
        \]
        于是
        \[
        \int \frac{\sqrt{1 + \sqrt[3]{x}}}{\sqrt[3]{x^2}} \, dx = 3 \int (1+\sqrt[3]{x})^{\frac{1}{2}} \, d(\sqrt[3]{x}) = 2(1+\sqrt[3]{x})^{\frac{3}{2}} + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int \frac{1}{x(1+x^{119})} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        分子分母同乘 $x^{118}$,构造 $\frac{f'}{f}$ 形式:
        \[
        I = \int \frac{1}{x(1+x^{119})} \, dx = \int \frac{x^{118}}{x^{119}(1+x^{119})} \, dx
        \]
        设 $t = x^{119}$,则 $dt = 119x^{118} \, dx$,
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \frac{1}{119} \int \frac{1}{t(1+t)} \, dt = \frac{1}{119} \int \left( \frac{1}{t} - \frac{1}{1+t} \right) \, dt \\
        &= \frac{1}{119} \ln\left| \frac{t}{1+t} \right| + C = \frac{1}{119} \ln\left| \frac{x^{119}}{1+x^{119}} \right| + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}
    
    \question  
    \[
    \int_{-1}^1 \frac{x^2}{1+2^x} \,dx 
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 \( u = -x \),则
        \[
        I = \int_{-1}^1 \frac{u^2}{1+2^{-u}}\,du = \int_{-1}^1 \frac{u^2 2^u}{1+2^u}\,du = \int_{-1}^1 \frac{x^2 2^x}{1+2^x}\,dx
        \]
        于是有
        \[
        2I = \int_{-1}^1 \frac{x^2(2^x + 1)}{1 + 2^x}\,dx = \int_{-1}^1 x^2\,dx = \frac{2}{3} \Rightarrow I = \frac{1}{3}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    I = \int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{2} \frac{\ln x}{1 + x^2} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        应用倒数代换法,设 $x = \dfrac{1}{t}$,则 $dx = -\dfrac{1}{t^2} \, dt$,整理得
        \[
        I = \int_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}} \frac{\ln\frac{1}{t}}{1 + (\frac{1}{t})^2} \left( -\frac{1}{t^2} \right) \, dt = \int_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}} \frac{\ln t}{t^2+1} \, dt = -I
        \]
        由于 $I = -I$,故 $I = 0$。
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\sqrt{3}} \frac{1}{1+x^2} \sin^{-1} \frac{2x}{1+x^2} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $x = \tan\theta$,则 $dx = \sec^2 \theta \, d\theta$。原积分变形为:
        \[
        I = \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} \sin^{-1}(\sin 2\theta) \, d\theta
        \]
        注意到 $\sin^{-1}(\sin \alpha)$ 的值取决于 $\alpha$ 所在的区间:当 $0 \le 2\theta \le \dfrac{\pi}{2}$,即 $0 \le \theta \le \dfrac{\pi}{4}$ 时,
        \[
        \sin^{-1}(\sin 2\theta) = 2\theta;
        \]
        当 $\dfrac{\pi}{2} < 2\theta \le \dfrac{2\pi}{3}$,即 $\dfrac{\pi}{4} < \theta \le \dfrac{\pi}{3}$ 时,
        \[
        \sin^{-1}(\sin 2\theta) = \pi - 2\theta
        \]
        因此,积分需分段计算:
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} 2\theta \, d\theta + \int_{\frac{\pi}{4}}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} (\pi - 2\theta) \, d\theta \\
        &= \Bigl[ \theta^2 \Bigr]_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} + \Bigl[ \pi\theta - \theta^2 \Bigr]_{\frac{\pi}{4}}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} \\
        &= \frac{\pi^2}{16} + \frac{2\pi^2}{9} - \frac{3\pi^2}{16} \\
        &= \frac{7\pi^2}{72}
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    I = \int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{2} \frac{x^{2012}-1}{x^{2014}+1} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $x = \dfrac{1}{t}$,则 $dx = -\dfrac{1}{t^2} \, dt$,代入积分得:
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}} \frac{\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{2012}-1}{\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{2014}+1} \left( -\frac{1}{t^2} \right) \, dt \\
        &= \int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{2} \frac{1-t^{2012}}{1+t^{2014}} \cdot \frac{t^{2014}}{t^{2012} \cdot t^2} \, dt \\
        &= -\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{2} \frac{t^{2012}-1}{t^{2014}+1} \, dt = -I
        \end{align*}
        由 $I = -I$ 可知 $I = 0$。
    \end{solution}

    \question 若正整数$k$满足
    \[
    \left(\int_0^1 x^{2018}(2019 + kx^{10})\sqrt{1 + x^{10}} \, dx\right)^2 = 8,
    \]
    求$k$的最大值。
    \begin{solution}
        由于被积函数随$k$严格递增,则当
        \[
        \int_0^1 x^{2018}(2019 + kx^{10})\sqrt{1 + x^{10}} \, dx = \sqrt{8}
        \]
        时$k$取最大。欲写成形如
        \[
        \int_0^1 (2019x^{2018-n} + kx^{2028-n}) \sqrt{x^{2n} + x^{2n+10}} \, dx
        \]
        的积分,考虑强迫
        \[
        2n-1 = 2018-n,
        \]
        可得$n = 673$,此时设$u=x^{1346}+x^{1356}$,则$du=1346x^{1345}+1356x^{1355} dx $,于是
        \begin{align*}
        I &=\int_0^1 (2019x^{1345} + kx^{1355}) \sqrt{x^{1346} + x^{1356}} \, dx \\
        &=\frac{3}{2}\int_0^1 (1346^{1345} + \frac{2}{3}kx^{1355}) \sqrt{x^{1346} + x^{1356}} \, dx
        \end{align*}
        当$\dfrac{2}{3}k=1356$即$k=2034$时,有
        \[
        I=\frac{3}{2}\int_0^2 \sqrt{u}\, du = \frac{3}{2}\left[\frac{2}{3}u^{\frac{3}{2}}\right]^2_0=\sqrt{8}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 设 $x$ 为正实数。
\begin{parts}
\part (a) (i) 设 $u = \sqrt{x} + \sqrt{\sqrt{x}}$。以 $u$ 表示 $\sqrt{x}$，进而以 $u$ 表示 $x$。
\begin{solution}
令 $v = \sqrt{\sqrt{x}}$，则 $v^2+v-u = 0$。
$v = \frac{-1 + \sqrt{1+4u}}{2}$。
$\sqrt{x} = v^2 = \frac{1+1+4u - 2\sqrt{1+4u}}{4} = \frac{1+2u - \sqrt{1+4u}}{2}$。
$x = \left(\frac{1+2u - \sqrt{1+4u}}{2}\right)^2$。
\end{solution}

\part (ii) 求 $\frac{d}{d\theta} \left(\sec \theta + \tan \theta\right) / \left(\sec \theta + \tan \theta\right)$。
\begin{solution}
令 $f(\theta) = \sec \theta + \tan \theta$。
$f'(\theta) = \sec \theta \tan \theta + \sec^2 \theta = \sec \theta(\tan \theta + \sec \theta)$。
所以原式等于 $\sec \theta$。
\end{solution}

\part (b) (i) 求 $\int \frac{du}{\sqrt{a^2 u^2 + 1}}$，其中 $a > 0$。
\begin{solution}
原式等于 $\frac{1}{a} \ln\left(au + \sqrt{a^2 u^2 + 1}\right) + C$。
\end{solution}

\part (ii) 利用先前结果，求 $\int \frac{dx}{\sqrt{x} + \sqrt{\sqrt{x}}}$。
\begin{solution}
令 $v = \sqrt{\sqrt{x}}$，$dx = 4v^3 dv$。
积分变为 $\int \frac{4v^3}{v^2+v} dv = 4 \int \frac{v^2}{v+1} dv = 4 \int \left(v - 1 + \frac{1}{v+1}\right) dv = 2v^2 - 4v + 4 \ln(v+1) + C$。
代入 $v = \sqrt[4]{x}$，即 $2\sqrt{x} - 4\sqrt[4]{x} + 4 \ln\left(\sqrt[4]{x} + 1\right) + C$。
\end{solution}

\part (c) (i) 分别解方程 $x + \sqrt{x} - 4 = 0$ 和 $x + \sqrt{x} - 1 = 0$。
\begin{solution}
对于 $x + \sqrt{x} - 4 = 0$，令 $w = \sqrt{x}$，$w^2+w-4=0$，$w = \frac{-1+\sqrt{17}}{2}$，$x = \frac{9-\sqrt{17}}{2}$。
对于 $x + \sqrt{x} - 1 = 0$，令 $w = \sqrt{x}$，$w^2+w-1=0$，$w = \frac{-1+\sqrt{5}}{2}$，$x = \frac{3-\sqrt{5}}{2}$。
\end{solution}

\part (ii) 计算 $\int_{\frac{3 - \sqrt{5}}{2}}^{\frac{9 - \sqrt{17}}{2}} \frac{1 + 2\sqrt{x}}{2\sqrt{x^2 + x\sqrt{x} + x\sqrt{x} + \sqrt{x}}} \, dx$。
\begin{solution}
令 $u = \sqrt{x}$，$du = \frac{1}{2\sqrt{x}} dx$，$dx = 2u \, du$。
积分变为 $\int \frac{1+2u}{2\sqrt{u^4+2u^3+u^2}} 2u \, du = \int \frac{2u+1}{u+1} du = \int \left(2 - \frac{1}{u+1}\right) du = 2u - \ln(u+1)$。
代入上下界计算得 $\ln 4$。
\end{solution}
\end{parts}
%部分分式
    \question 
    \[
    \int \frac{dx}{x^3 + 1}
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        部分分式分解得
        \[
        \frac{1}{x^3 + 1}
        = \frac{1}{3(x + 1)} + \frac{-\frac{1}{3}x + \frac{2}{3}}{x^2 - x + 1}
        \]
        于是
        \[
        \int \frac{dx}{x^3 + 1}
        = \frac{1}{3} \int \frac{dx}{x + 1}
        - \frac{1}{3} \int \frac{x}{x^2 - x + 1} dx
        + \frac{2}{3} \int \frac{dx}{x^2 - x + 1}
        \]
        其中
        \[
        \int \frac{dx}{x + 1} = \ln|x + 1|,\quad
        \int \frac{x}{x^2 - x + 1} dx = \frac{1}{2} \ln(x^2 - x + 1)
        \]
        \[
        \int \frac{dx}{x^2 - x + 1}
        = \int \frac{dx}{\left(x - \frac{1}{2}\right)^2 + \left(\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)^2}
        = \frac{2}{\sqrt{3}} \arctan\left( \frac{2x - 1}{\sqrt{3}} \right) + C
        \]
        故
        \[
        \int \frac{dx}{x^3 + 1}
        = \frac{1}{3} \ln|x + 1|
        - \frac{1}{6} \ln(x^2 - x + 1)
        + \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \arctan\left( \frac{2x - 1}{\sqrt{3}} \right) + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int \frac{x^4+1}{x^6+1} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        注意到 $x^6+1 = (x^2+1)(x^4-x^2+1)$,将被积函数分子进行配凑:
        \[
        \frac{x^4+1}{x^6+1} = \frac{(x^4-x^2+1) + x^2}{x^6+1} = \frac{1}{x^2+1} + \frac{x^2}{x^6+1}
        \]
        于是积分拆分为:
        \[
        I = \int \frac{x^4+1}{x^6+1} \, dx = \int \frac{1}{x^2+1} \, dx + \int \frac{x^2}{(x^3)^2+1} \, dx
        \]
        第一部分为 $\tan^{-1} x$；第二部分通过 $d(x^3) = 3x^2 \, dx$ 得:
        \[
        \frac{1}{3} \int \frac{d(x^3)}{(x^3)^2+1} = \frac{1}{3} \tan^{-1}(x^3)
        \]
        故结果为:
        \[
        I = \tan^{-1} x + \frac{1}{3} \tan^{-1}(x^3) + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int_{0}^{1} \frac{x^4(1-x)^4}{x^2+1} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        进行多项式除法,
        \[
        \frac{x^4(1-x)^4}{x^2+1}=\frac{x^8-4x^7+6x^6-4x^5+x^4}{x^2+1} = x^6 - 4x^5 + 5x^4 - 4x^2 + 4 - \frac{4}{x^2+1}
        \]
        逐项积分得
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int_{0}^{1} \left( x^6 - 4x^5 + 5x^4 - 4x^2 + 4 - \frac{4}{x^2+1} \right) \, dx \\
        &= \left[ \frac{1}{7}x^7 - \frac{2}{3}x^6 + x^5 - \frac{4}{3}x^3 + 4x - 4\tan^{-1}x \right]_{0}^{1} \\
        &= \frac{22}{7} - \pi
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_{0}^{1} \frac{(x^2+1)(x^2+4)}{(x^2+3)(x^2-4)} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        将被积函数化为真分式:
        \[
        \frac{x^4+5x^2+4}{x^4-x^2-12} = 1 + \frac{6x^2+16}{(x^2+3)(x-2)(x+2)}
        \]
        对真分式部分设待定系数:
        \[
        \frac{6x^2+16}{(x^2+3)(x-2)(x+2)} = \frac{Ax+B}{x^2+3} + \frac{C}{x-2} + \frac{D}{x+2}
        \]
        两边同乘以分母得
        \[
        6x^2+16 = (Ax+B)(x^2-4) + C(x^2+3)(x+2) + D(x^2+3)(x-2)
        \]
        利用特殊值法求解:
        \begin{itemize}
        \item 令 $x = 2$:$6(4)+16 = C(7)(4) \Rightarrow C = \dfrac{10}{7}$。
        \item 令 $x = -2$:$6(4)+16 = D(7)(-4) \Rightarrow D = -\dfrac{10}{7}$。
        \item 令 $x = 0$:$16 = B(-4) + 2(3)C - 2(3)D\Rightarrow B = \dfrac{2}{7}$。
        \item 比较 $x^3$ 项系数:$0 = A + C + D$,故 $A = 0$。
        \end{itemize}
        于是被积函数为
        \[
        1 + \frac{2}{7(x^2+3)} + \frac{10}{7(x-2)} - \frac{10}{7(x+2)}
        \]
        积分得
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \left[ x + \frac{2}{7\sqrt{3}}\tan^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{\sqrt{3}}\right) + \frac{10}{7}\ln\left| \frac{x-2}{x+2} \right| \right]_{0}^{1} \\
        &= \left( 1 + \frac{2}{7\sqrt{3}} \cdot \frac{\pi}{6} + \frac{10}{7}\ln\frac{1}{3} \right) - 0 \\
        &= 1 + \frac{\pi}{21\sqrt{3}} - \frac{10}{7}\ln 3
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{1}{3}} \frac{32x^2}{(x^2-1)(x+1)^3} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        化简分母得 $\dfrac{32x^2}{(x-1)(x+1)^4}$。设 $u = x+1$,则 $dx = du$,于是
        \[
        I = \int_{1}^{\frac{4}{3}} \frac{32(u-1)^2}{(u-2)u^4} \, du = \int_{1}^{\frac{4}{3}} \frac{32u^2-64u+32}{u^4(u-2)} \, du
        \]
        进行部分分式分解:
        \[
        \frac{32u^2-64u+32}{u^4(u-2)} = -\frac{16}{u^4} + \frac{24}{u^3} - \frac{4}{u^2} - \frac{2}{u} + \frac{2}{u-2}
        \]
        逐项积分得
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \left[ \frac{16}{3u^3} - \frac{12}{u^2} + \frac{4}{u} - 2\ln|u| + 2\ln|u-2| \right]_{1}^{\frac{4}{3}} \\
        &= \left( \frac{9}{4} - \frac{27}{4} + 3 - 2\ln\frac{4}{3} + 2\ln\frac{2}{3} \right) - \left( \frac{16}{3} - 12 + 4 \right) \\
        &= \frac{7}{6} - 2\ln 2
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int \frac{1}{\sqrt[4]{1+x^4}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $(tx)^4 = 1+x^4$,则 $x = (t^4-1)^{-\frac{1}{4}}$,对 $x$ 求导得 $dx = -t^3(t^4-1)^{-\frac{5}{4}} \, dt$,代入得
        \[
        I = \int \frac{1}{tx} \cdot dx = \int (t^4-1)^{\frac{1}{4}} \cdot \frac{1}{t} \cdot [-t^3(t^4-1)^{-\frac{5}{4}}] \, dt = -\int \frac{t^2}{t^4-1} \, dt
        \]
        利用部分分式拆分,
        \[
        I = -\frac{1}{2} \int \left( \frac{1}{t^2-1} + \frac{1}{t^2+1} \right) \, dt = -\frac{1}{4} \ln\left| \frac{t-1}{t+1} \right| - \frac{1}{2} \tan^{-1} t + C
        \]
        其中 
        \[
        t = \frac{\sqrt[4]{1+x^4}}{x}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{2}{\sqrt{x} - 3\sqrt[4]{x} + 2} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $t = \sqrt[4]{x}$,则 $x = t^4, \, dx = 4t^3 \, dt$。
        \[
        I = \int \frac{2}{\sqrt{x} - 3\sqrt[4]{x} + 2} \, dx = \int \frac{8t^3}{t^2 - 3t + 2} \, dt
        \]
        进行多项式除法与部分分式分解,可得
        \[
        \frac{8t^3}{(t-1)(t-2)} = 8t + 24 + \frac{64}{t-2} - \frac{8}{t-1}
        \]
        逐项积分得:
        \begin{align*}
        I &= 4t^2 + 24t + 64 \ln|t-2| - 8 \ln|t-1| + C \\
        &= 4\sqrt{x} + 24\sqrt[4]{x} + 64 \ln|\sqrt[4]{x}-2| - 8 \ln|\sqrt[4]{x}-1| + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{3x^3}{x^4 + x^3 + x + 1} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        观察到
        \[
        x^4+x^3+x+1 = (x^3+1)(x+1) = (x+1)^2(x^2-x+1)
        \]
        设待定系数:
        \[
        \frac{3x^3}{(x+1)^2(x^2-x+1)} = \frac{A}{x+1} + \frac{B}{(x+1)^2} + \frac{Cx+D}{x^2-x+1}
        \]
        求得 $A=2, B=-1, C=1, D=-1$,
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int \frac{2}{x+1} \, dx - \int \frac{1}{(x+1)^2} \, dx + \int \frac{x-1}{x^2-x+1} \, dx \\
        &= 2\ln|x+1| + \frac{1}{x+1} + \frac{1}{2}\ln(x^2-x+1) - \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\tan^{-1}\left(\frac{2x-1}{\sqrt{3}}\right) + C \\
        &= \ln\left|(x+1)^2 \sqrt{x^2-x+1}\right| - \frac{\sqrt{3}}{3} \tan^{-1}\left(\frac{2x-1}{\sqrt{3}}\right) + \frac{1}{x+1} + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{x^2(x^4+1)}{\sqrt{x^4+2}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        注意到
        \[
        I =\int \frac{x^2(x^4+1)}{\sqrt{x^4+2}} \, dx = \int \frac{x^6+x^2}{\sqrt{x^4+2}} \, dx = \int \frac{x^7+x^3}{\sqrt{x^8+2x^4}} \, dx
        \]
        此时有 $\dfrac{d}{dx}(x^8+2x^4) = 8x^7+8x^3 = 8(x^7+x^3)$,于是
        \[
        I = \frac{1}{8} \int \frac{d(x^8+2x^4)}{\sqrt{x^8+2x^4}} = \frac{1}{4}\sqrt{x^8+2x^4} + C = \frac{x^2}{4}\sqrt{x^4+2} + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{x^2+1}{(x^2-2x+2)^3} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $x-1 = \tan \theta$,则 $dx = \sec^2 \theta \, d\theta,x^2-2x+2 = \sec^2 \theta$,代入得:
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int \frac{\tan^2\theta + 2\tan\theta + 2}{\sec^6\theta} \sec^2\theta \, d\theta = \int (\sin^2\theta\cos^2\theta + 2\sin\theta\cos^3\theta + 2\cos^4\theta) \, d\theta \\
        &= \int \left[ \frac{1}{4}\sin^2 2\theta + 2\sin\theta\cos^3\theta + 2\left(\frac{1+\cos 2\theta}{2}\right)^2 \right] \, \\
        &= \int \left[ \frac{1}{8} - \frac{1}{8}\cos 4\theta + 2\sin\theta\cos^3\theta + \frac{1}{2} + \cos 2\theta + \frac{1}{4}(1+\cos 4\theta) \right] \, d\theta \\
        &= \int \left[ \frac{7}{8} + \cos 2\theta + \frac{1}{8}\cos 4\theta + 2\sin\theta\cos^3\theta \right] \, d\theta \\
        &= \frac{7}{8}\theta + \frac{1}{2}\sin 2\theta + \frac{1}{32}\sin 4\theta - \frac{1}{2}\cos^4\theta + C
        \end{align*}
        还原变量:由 $\tan \theta = x-1$,知 
        \[
        \cos^2 \theta = \frac{1}{1+\tan^2 \theta} = \frac{1}{x^2-2x+2}
        \]
        利用倍角公式:
        \begin{itemize}
        \item $\sin 2\theta = 2\sin\theta\cos\theta = 2\tan\theta\cos^2\theta = \dfrac{2(x-1)}{x^2-2x+2}$
        \item $\sin 4\theta = 2\sin 2\theta(2\cos^2\theta-1) = \dfrac{4(x-1)(2x-x^2)}{(x^2-2x+2)^2}$
        \item $\cos^4 \theta = \left(\dfrac{1}{x^2-2x+2}\right)^2$
        \end{itemize}
        代入整理得
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \frac{7}{8} \tan^{-1}(x-1) + \frac{x-1}{x^2-2x+2} + \frac{(x-1)(2x-x^2)}{8(x^2-2x+2)^2} - \frac{4}{8(x^2-2x+2)^2} + C \\
        &= \frac{7}{8} \tan^{-1}(x-1) + \frac{7x^3-21x^2+30x-20}{8(x^2-2x+2)^2} + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int \frac{dx}{\sqrt{x+1} + \sqrt[4]{x+1}}
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $x+1 = u^4$,则 $dx = 4u^3 \, du$。原积分变为:
        \begin{align*}
        \int \frac{dx}{\sqrt{x+1} + \sqrt[4]{x+1}} 
        &= \int \frac{4u^3}{u^2+u} \, du \\
        &= 4 \int \frac{u^2}{u+1} \, du \\
        &= 4 \int \left(u - 1 + \frac{1}{u+1}\right) \, du  \\
        &= 2u^2 - 4u + 4\ln(u+1) + C \\
        &= 2\sqrt{x+1} - 4\sqrt[4]{x+1} + 4\ln(\sqrt[4]{x+1}+1) + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{1+x^2}{x\sqrt{x^4-x^2+1}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        分子分母同时除以 $x^2$:
        \[
        I = \int \frac{1+x^2}{x\sqrt{x^4-x^2+1}} \, dx = \int \frac{1 + \frac{1}{x^2}}{\sqrt{(x - \frac{1}{x})^2 + 1}} \, dx
        \]
        设 $t = x - \frac{1}{x}$,则 $dt = (1 + \frac{1}{x^2}) \, dx$,
        \[
        I = \int \frac{dt}{\sqrt{t^2+1}} = \ln|t + \sqrt{t^2+1}| + C  = \ln\left| \frac{x^2-1 + \sqrt{x^4-x^2+1}}{x} \right| + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{1}{(2x^2+3)\sqrt{5x^2+4}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $x = \dfrac{1}{u}$,则 $dx = -\dfrac{1}{u^2} \, du$,
        \[
        I = \int \frac{-\frac{1}{u^2} \, du}{(\frac{2}{u^2}+3)\sqrt{\frac{5}{u^2}+4}} = \int \frac{-u \, du}{(2+3u^2)\sqrt{5+4u^2}}
        \]
        设 $t = \sqrt{5+4u^2}$,则 $u^2 = \dfrac{t^2-5}{4},t \, dt = 4u \, du$。
        代入 $2+3u^2 = 2 + 3\left(\dfrac{t^2-5}{4}\right) = \dfrac{3t^2-7}{4}$:
        \[
        I = \int \frac{-\frac{1}{4}t \, dt}{\frac{3t^2-7}{4} \cdot t} = -\int \frac{dt}{3t^2-7} = \frac{1}{3} \int \frac{dt}{\frac{7}{3}-t^2}
        \]
        利用公式 
        \[
        \int \frac{dx}{a^2-x^2} = \frac{1}{2a}\ln\left|\frac{a+x}{a-x}\right|
        \]
        有
        \[
        I = \frac{1}{3} \cdot \frac{1}{2\sqrt{\frac{7}{3}}} \ln\left| \frac{\sqrt{\frac{7}{3}}+t}{\sqrt{\frac{7}{3}}-t} \right| + C = \frac{\sqrt{21}}{42} \ln\left| \frac{\sqrt{7}+\sqrt{3}t}{\sqrt{7}-\sqrt{3}t} \right| + C, \quad t = \sqrt{5+\frac{4}{x^2}}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{x+x^{\frac{2}{3}}+x^{\frac{1}{6}}}{x(1+x^{\frac{1}{3}})} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $x = u^6$,则 $dx = 6u^5 \, du$,代入原积分:
        \begin{align*}
        \int \frac{x+x^{\frac{2}{3}}+x^{\frac{1}{6}}}{x(1+x^{\frac{1}{3}})} \, dx 
        &= \int \frac{u^6+u^4+u}{u^6(1+u^2)} \cdot 6u^5 \, du \\
        &= 6 \int \frac{u^5+u^3+1}{1+u^2} \, du \\
        &= 6 \int \left( u^3 + \frac{1}{1+u^2} \right) \, du \\
        &= 6 \left( \frac{1}{4}u^4 + \tan^{-1} u \right) + C \\
        &= \frac{3}{2}x^{\frac{2}{3}} + 6\tan^{-1}(x^{\frac{1}{6}}) + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{\sqrt[3]{1+\sqrt[4]{x}}}{\sqrt{x}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $1+x^{\frac{1}{4}} = t^3$,则 $x = (t^3-1)^4$,且$dx = 12t^2(t^3-1)^3 \, dt$:
        \[
        I = \int \frac{t}{(t^3-1)^2} \cdot 12t^2(t^3-1)^3 \, dt = 12 \int (t^6-t^3) \, dt
        \]
        积分得:
        \[
        I = \frac{12}{7}t^7 - 3t^4 + C = \frac{12}{7}(1+\sqrt[4]{x})^{\frac{7}{3}} - 3(1+\sqrt[4]{x})^{\frac{4}{3}} + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{x-1}{(x+1)\sqrt{x^3+x^2+x}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        分子分母同乘 $(x+1)$ 构造如下:
        \[
        I = \int \frac{x^2-1}{(x+1)^2 \sqrt{x^3+x^2+x}} \, dx = \int \frac{1-\frac{1}{x^2}}{(x+2+\frac{1}{x})\sqrt{x+1+\frac{1}{x}}} \, dx
        \]
        此时可设 $u = \sqrt{x+1+\dfrac{1}{x}}$,则 
        \[
        u^2 = x+1+\frac{1}{x} \Rightarrow 2u \, du = \left(1-\frac{1}{x^2}\right) \, dx,
        \]
        代入得
        \[
        I = \int \frac{2u \, du}{(u^2+1) \cdot u} = 2 \int \frac{du}{u^2+1} = 2\tan^{-1} u + C = 2 \tan^{-1} \sqrt{x+1+\frac{1}{x}} + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{x}{(x-1)\sqrt{x^2-2x}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $x-1 = t$,则 $dx = dt,x^2-2x = t^2-1$,原积分变为
        \[
        I = \int \frac{t+1}{t\sqrt{t^2-1}} \, dt = \int \frac{dt}{\sqrt{t^2-1}} + \int \frac{dt}{t\sqrt{t^2-1}}
        \]
        第一项为 $\ln|t+\sqrt{t^2-1}|$；第二项令 $t = \sec \theta$ 则 $dt = \sec \theta \tan \theta \, d\theta$,且 $\sqrt{t^2-1} = \tan \theta$:
        \[
        \int \frac{\sec \theta \tan \theta}{\sec \theta \tan \theta} \, d\theta = \int 1 \, d\theta = \theta + C_2 
        \]
        故
        \[
        I = \ln|t+\sqrt{t^2-1}| + \sec^{-1}|t| + C
        = \ln|x-1+\sqrt{x^2-2x}| + \sec^{-1}|x-1| + C
        \]
    \end{solution}
\question
设 $I(x) = \int \frac{dx}{(x-11)^{\frac{11}{13}}(x+15)^{\frac{15}{13}}}$。若 $I(37) - I(24) = \frac{1}{4} \left( \frac{1}{b^{\frac{1}{13}}} - \frac{1}{c^{\frac{1}{13}}} \right)$，其中 $b, c \in N$，则 $3(b + c)$ 等于：
(1) 22 \quad (2) 39 \quad (3) 40 \quad (4) 26

\begin{solution}
观察指数之和：$\frac{11}{13} + \frac{15}{13} = 2$。对被积函数进行如下变形：
\[
I(x) = \int \frac{1}{(x+15)^{\frac{11}{13} + \frac{15}{13}} \cdot \left( \frac{x-11}{x+15} \right)^{\frac{11}{13}}} \,dx = \int \frac{1}{(x+15)^2} \left( \frac{x-11}{x+15} \right)^{-\frac{11}{13}} \,dx
\]
设 $t = \frac{x-11}{x+15}$，则其导数为：
\[
dt = \frac{(x+15) - (x-11)}{(x+15)^2} \,dx = \frac{26}{(x+15)^2} \,dx
\]
代入积分式：
\[
I(x) = \frac{1}{26} \int t^{-\frac{11}{13}} \,dt = \frac{1}{26} \cdot \frac{13}{2} t^{\frac{2}{13}} + C = \frac{1}{4} \left( \frac{x-11}{x+15} \right)^{\frac{2}{13}} + C
\]
计算 $I(37) - I(24)$：
对于 $x = 37$，$t = \frac{37-11}{37+15} = \frac{26}{52} = \frac{1}{2}$。
对于 $x = 24$，$t = \frac{24-11}{24+15} = \frac{13}{39} = \frac{1}{3}$。
\[
I(37) - I(24) = \frac{1}{4} \left[ \left( \frac{1}{2} \right)^{\frac{2}{13}} - \left( \frac{1}{3} \right)^{\frac{2}{13}} \right] = \frac{1}{4} \left( \frac{1}{4^{\frac{1}{13}}} - \frac{1}{9^{\frac{1}{13}}} \right)
\]
对照题目所给形式，得 $b = 4, c = 9$。
最后计算：
\[
3(b + c) = 3(4 + 9) = 3 \times 13 = 39
\]
故正确选项为 (2)。
\end{solution}
    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{3\sin 2x + 4\cos x e^{\sin x} + 8\cos x}{3\sin x + e^{\sin x} + 1} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        利用二倍角公式 $\sin 2x = 2\sin x \cos x$ 并提取分子中的 $\cos x$:
        \[
        I = \int \frac{(6\sin x + 4e^{\sin x} + 8)\cos x}{3\sin x + e^{\sin x} + 1} \, dx
        \]
        令 $u = \sin x$,则 $du = \cos x \, dx$。代入原式:
        \[
        I = \int \frac{6u + 4e^u + 8}{3u + e^u + 1} \, du = 2 \int \frac{3u + 2e^u + 4}{3u + e^u + 1} \, du
        \]
        观察分母 $f(u) = 3u + e^u + 1$,其导数为 $f'(u) = e^u + 3$,于是
        \begin{align*}
        I &= 2 \left( \int \frac{3u + e^u + 1}{3u + e^u + 1} \, du + \int \frac{e^u + 3}{3u + e^u + 1} \, du \right) \\
        &= 2u + 2\ln|3u + e^u + 1| + C \\
        &= 2\sin x + 2\ln|3\sin x + e^{\sin x} + 1| + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

\question 
    \[
    \int \frac{\sqrt{x}}{\sqrt[3]{x}-1} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $x = u^6$,则 $dx = 6u^5 \, du$。原积分变为:
        \[
        I = \int \frac{u^3}{u^2-1} \cdot 6u^5 \, du = 6 \int \frac{u^8}{u^2-1} \, du
        \]
        进行多项式除法:
        \[
        \frac{u^8}{u^2-1} = u^6+u^4+u^2+1 + \frac{1}{u^2-1}
        \]
        逐项积分得
        \begin{align*}
        I &= 6 \left( \frac{u^7}{7} + \frac{u^5}{5} + \frac{u^3}{3} + u + \frac{1}{2}\ln\left|\frac{u-1}{u+1}\right| \right) + C \\
        &= \frac{6}{7}x^{\frac{7}{6}} + \frac{6}{5}x^{\frac{5}{6}} + 2x^{\frac{1}{2}} + 6x^{\frac{1}{6}} + 3\ln\left|\frac{x^{\frac{1}{6}}-1}{x^{\frac{1}{6}}+1}\right| + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
对于 $\alpha, \beta, \gamma, \delta \in N$，若 $\int \left( \left(\frac{x}{e}\right)^{2x} + \left(\frac{e}{x}\right)^{2x} \right) \log_e x \,dx = \frac{1}{\alpha} \left(\frac{x}{e}\right)^{\beta x} - \frac{1}{\gamma} \left(\frac{e}{x}\right)^{\delta x} + C$，其中 $e = \sum_{n=0}^{\infty} \frac{1}{n!}$，$C$ 为积分常数，则 $\alpha + 2\beta + 3\gamma - 4\delta$ 等于：
(1) 1 \quad (2) 4 \quad (3) -4 \quad (4) -8

\begin{solution}
令 $t = \left(\frac{x}{e}\right)^x$，则 $\ln t = x \ln x - x$。
两边对 $x$ 求导：
\[
\frac{1}{t} \frac{dt}{dx} = \ln x + x \cdot \frac{1}{x} - 1 = \ln x \implies dt = \left(\frac{x}{e}\right)^x \ln x \,dx
\]
原积分可以改写为：
\[
I = \int \left[ \left(\frac{x}{e}\right)^x + \left(\frac{x}{e}\right)^{-3x} \right] \cdot \left( \left(\frac{x}{e}\right)^x \ln x \right) \,dx
\]
代入 $t$ 进行换元：
\[
I = \int (t + t^{-3}) \,dt = \frac{t^2}{2} + \frac{t^{-2}}{-2} + C
\]
将 $t = \left(\frac{x}{e}\right)^x$ 代回：
\[
I = \frac{1}{2} \left(\frac{x}{e}\right)^{2x} - \frac{1}{2} \left(\frac{x}{e}\right)^{-2x} + C = \frac{1}{2} \left(\frac{x}{e}\right)^{2x} - \frac{1}{2} \left(\frac{e}{x}\right)^{2x} + C
\]
对照题目给出的形式：
$\alpha = 2, \beta = 2, \gamma = 2, \delta = 2$。
计算所求表达式的值：
\[
\alpha + 2\beta + 3\gamma - 4\delta = 2 + 2(2) + 3(2) - 4(2) = 2 + 4 + 6 - 8 = 4
\]
故正确选项为 (2)。
\end{solution}
    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{(x+1)^3(x-1)}{(x^2+1)^2 \sqrt{x^4+x^2+1}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        分子写成
        \[
        (x+1)^2(x+1)(x-1) = (x^2+2x+1)(x^2-1)
        \]
        于是
        \[
        I = \int \frac{(1+\frac{2}{x}+\frac{1}{x^2})(1-\frac{1}{x^2})}{(1+\frac{1}{x^2})^2 \sqrt{1+\frac{1}{x^2}+\frac{1}{x^4}}} \, dx = \int \frac{(x+\frac{1}{x}+2)(1-\frac{1}{x^2})}{(x+\frac{1}{x})^2 \sqrt{(x+\frac{1}{x})^2-1}} 
        \]
        设 $t = x+\dfrac{1}{x}$,则 $dt = (1-\dfrac{1}{x^2})dx$,原式化为:
        \[
        I = \int \frac{t+2}{t^2\sqrt{t^2-1}} \, dt = \int \frac{1}{t\sqrt{t^2-1}} \, dt + 2 \int \frac{1}{t^2\sqrt{t^2-1}} \, dt
        \]
        第一部分设 $t=\sec \theta \Rightarrow I_1 = \sec^{-1}|t| + C$,第二部分设 $t=\frac{1}{\cos \theta}$ 或 $u=\frac{1}{t}$ 还原得 $I_2 = \frac{2\sqrt{t^2-1}}{t} + C$。代回 $t = x+\frac{1}{x}$:
        \[
        I = \sec^{-1}\left|x+\frac{1}{x}\right| + \frac{2\sqrt{x^4+x^2+1}}{x^2+1} + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_0^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{e^x(\sin x + \cos x - 2)}{(\cos x - 2)^2} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        有\[\int_0^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{e^x(\sin x + \cos x - 2)}{(\cos x - 2)^2} \, dx 
        =\int_0^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e^{x}\left(\frac{\sin x}{(\cos x-2)^{2}}+\frac{1}{\cos x-2}\right) \, dx 
        \]
        发现被积函数形如 \(e^{x}(f(x)+f^{\prime }(x))\),其中\[
        f(x)= \frac{1}{\cos x-2}, f^{\prime }(x)=\frac{\sin x}{(\cos x-2)^{2}}
        \]
        所以\[
        \int _{0}^{\frac{\pi }{2}}e^{x}\left(\frac{1}{\cos x-2}+\frac{\sin x}{(\cos x-2)^{2}}\right)dx=\left[\frac{e^{x}}{\cos x-2}\right]_{0}^{\frac{\pi }{2}}=1 - \frac{e^{\frac{\pi}{2}}}{2}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int e^x \left( \frac{1}{\sqrt{x^2+1}} + \frac{1-2x^2}{(x^2+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}} \right) dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        观察被积函数,尝试配凑 $e^x(f(x) + f'(x))$ 的形式。
        令 
        \[
        f(x) = \frac{1}{\sqrt{x^2+1}} + \frac{x}{(x^2+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}
        \]
        计算其导数:
        \begin{align*}
        f'(x) &= -\frac{1}{2}(x^2+1)^{-\frac{3}{2}}(2x) + \left[ 1 \cdot (x^2+1)^{-\frac{3}{2}} + x \cdot \left(-\frac{3}{2}\right)(x^2+1)^{-\frac{5}{2}}(2x) \right] \\
        &= -\frac{x}{(x^2+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}} + \frac{1}{(x^2+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}} - \frac{3x^2}{(x^2+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}} \\
        &= -\frac{x}{(x^2+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}} + \frac{1-2x^2}{(x^2+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}}
        \end{align*}
        于是原被积函数可改写为:
        \[
        e^x \left[ \left( \frac{1}{\sqrt{x^2+1}} + \frac{x}{(x^2+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}} \right) + \left( -\frac{x}{(x^2+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}} + \frac{1-2x^2}{(x^2+1)^{\frac{5}{2}}} \right) \right] = e^x [f(x) + f'(x)]
        \]
        根据积分性质 
        \[
        \int e^x [f(x) + f'(x)] \, dx = e^x f(x) + C,
        \]
        得
        \[
        I = e^x \left( \frac{1}{\sqrt{x^2+1}} + \frac{x}{(x^2+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}} \right) + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
设 $S(x)$ 和 $C(x)$ 为定义在 $\mathbb{R}$ 上的可逆函数，其中 $S(0) = 0$ 且 $C(0) = 1$。
已知 $\frac{d}{dx} S(x) = [C(x)]^2$ 且 $\frac{d}{dx} C(x) = -[S(x)]^2$。
\begin{parts}
\part
求 $\frac{d}{dx} \left\{ [S(x)]^3 + [C(x)]^3 \right\}$。从而求 $[S(x)]^3 + [C(x)]^3$。
\begin{solution}
根据链式法则，我们有：
\[
\frac{d}{dx} \left\{ [S(x)]^3 + [C(x)]^3 \right\} = 3[S(x)]^2 S'(x) + 3[C(x)]^2 C'(x)
\]
代入已知导数：
\[
= 3[S(x)]^2 [C(x)]^2 + 3[C(x)]^2 \left(-[S(x)]^2\right) = 0
\]
因为导数为 0，所以该表达式为常数，设为 $K$。
代入 $x=0$：
\[
K = [S(0)]^3 + [C(0)]^3 = 0^3 + 1^3 = 1
\]
因此可得：
\[
[S(x)]^3 + [C(x)]^3 = 1
\]
\end{solution}
\part
仅用 $C(x)$ 表示求 $\frac{d}{dx} [S(x)C(x)]$。并求 $\frac{d}{dx} \left[ \frac{S(x)}{C(x)} \right]$ 和 $\frac{d}{dx} \left[ \frac{C(x)}{S(x)} \right]$。
\begin{solution}
使用乘积法则：
\[
\frac{d}{dx} [S(x)C(x)] = S'(x)C(x) + S(x)C'(x) = [C(x)]^3 - [S(x)]^3
\]
由 (a) 可知 $[S(x)]^3 = 1 - [C(x)]^3$，代入得：
\[
\frac{d}{dx} [S(x)C(x)] = [C(x)]^3 - \left(1 - [C(x)]^3\right) = 2[C(x)]^3 - 1
\]
使用商法则求第二个导数：
\[
\frac{d}{dx} \left[ \frac{S(x)}{C(x)} \right] = \frac{S'(x)C(x) - S(x)C'(x)}{[C(x)]^2} = \frac{[C(x)]^3 + [S(x)]^3}{[C(x)]^2} = \frac{1}{[C(x)]^2}
\]
同理求第三个导数：
\[
\frac{d}{dx} \left[ \frac{C(x)}{S(x)} \right] = \frac{C'(x)S(x) - C(x)S'(x)}{[S(x)]^2} = \frac{-[S(x)]^3 - [C(x)]^3}{[S(x)]^2} = \frac{-1}{[S(x)]^2}
\]
\end{solution}
\part
求下列积分：
(i) $\int [S(x)]^3 \,dx$ ，$\int [C(x)]^3 \,dx$ ，$\int [S(x)]^5 \,dx$。
(ii) $\int \frac{\,dx}{\left(1-x^3\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}}$ ，$\int \frac{\,dx}{\left(1-x^3\right)^{\frac{4}{3}}}$ ，$\int \sqrt[3]{1-x^3} \,dx$。
\begin{solution}
对于 (i)：
由于 $\frac{d}{dx}[S(x)C(x)] = 1 - 2[S(x)]^3$，可得：
\[
[S(x)]^3 = \frac{1}{2} - \frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}[S(x)C(x)]
\]
积分得：
\[
\int [S(x)]^3 \,dx = \frac{x}{2} - \frac{1}{2}S(x)C(x) + C_1
\]
同理对于 $\int [C(x)]^3 \,dx$：
\[
\int [C(x)]^3 \,dx = \int \left(1 - [S(x)]^3\right) \,dx = \frac{x}{2} + \frac{1}{2}S(x)C(x) + C_2
\]
对于 $\int [S(x)]^5 \,dx$：
\[
\int [S(x)]^5 \,dx = \int \left(1 - [C(x)]^3\right) \left(-C'(x)\right) \,dx
\]
\[
= \int \left(-C'(x) + [C(x)]^3 C'(x)\right) \,dx = -C(x) + \frac{[C(x)]^4}{4} + C_3
\]

对于 (ii)：
令 $x = S(t)$，则 $dx = [C(t)]^2 \,dt$，且 $1 - x^3 = [C(t)]^3$，$t = S^{-1}(x)$。
第一个积分：
\[
\int \frac{\,dx}{\left(1-x^3\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}} = \int \frac{[C(t)]^2 \,dt}{\left([C(t)]^3\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}} = \int 1 \,dt = S^{-1}(x) + C_4
\]
第二个积分：
\[
\int \frac{\,dx}{\left(1-x^3\right)^{\frac{4}{3}}} = \int \frac{[C(t)]^2 \,dt}{\left([C(t)]^3\right)^{\frac{4}{3}}} = \int \frac{1}{[C(t)]^2} \,dt
\]
由 (b) 可知上式为：
\[
= \frac{S(t)}{C(t)} + C_5 = \frac{x}{\sqrt[3]{1-x^3}} + C_5
\]
第三个积分：
\[
\int \sqrt[3]{1-x^3} \,dx = \int \sqrt[3]{[C(t)]^3} [C(t)]^2 \,dt = \int [C(t)]^3 \,dt
\]
利用 (i) 的结果：
\[
= \frac{t}{2} + \frac{1}{2}S(t)C(t) + C_6 = \frac{1}{2}S^{-1}(x) + \frac{1}{2}x\sqrt[3]{1-x^3} + C_6
\]
\end{solution}
\end{parts}

    \question
若 $\int \frac{(x^2+1)e^x}{(x+1)^2} \,dx = f(x)e^x + C$，其中 $C$ 为常数，则 $\frac{d^3f}{dx^3}$ 在 $x = 1$ 处的值等于：
(1) $\frac{3}{4}$ \quad (2) $\frac{3}{8}$ \quad (3) $-\frac{3}{2}$ \quad (4) $\frac{7}{8}$

\begin{solution}
首先处理被积函数，尝试构造 $e^x [g(x) + g'(x)]$ 的形式。
分子 $x^2 + 1$ 可以改写为 $(x^2 - 1) + 2$：
\[
\int e^x \left[ \frac{x^2 - 1 + 2}{(x+1)^2} \right] \,dx = \int e^x \left[ \frac{(x-1)(x+1)}{(x+1)^2} + \frac{2}{(x+1)^2} \right] \,dx
\]
简化得：
\[
\int e^x \left[ \frac{x-1}{x+1} + \frac{2}{(x+1)^2} \right] \,dx
\]
设 $g(x) = \frac{x-1}{x+1}$，则其导数为：
\[
g'(x) = \frac{1(x+1) - 1(x-1)}{(x+1)^2} = \frac{2}{(x+1)^2}
\]
根据积分公式 $\int e^x [g(x) + g'(x)] \,dx = e^x g(x) + C$，可知：
\[
f(x) = \frac{x-1}{x+1} = \frac{x+1-2}{x+1} = 1 - \frac{2}{x+1}
\]
对 $f(x)$ 求三阶导数：
\[
f'(x) = 2(x+1)^{-2}
\]
\[
f''(x) = -4(x+1)^{-3}
\]
\[
f'''(x) = 12(x+1)^{-4}
\]
当 $x = 1$ 时：
\[
f'''(1) = \frac{12}{(1+1)^4} = \frac{12}{16} = \frac{3}{4}
\]
故正确选项为 (1)。
\end{solution}
\question
若 $\int e^x \left( \frac{x \sin^{-1} x}{\sqrt{1-x^2}} + \frac{\sin^{-1} x}{(1-x^2)^{3/2}} + \frac{x}{1-x^2} \right) \,dx = g(x) + C$，则 $g\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)$ 等于：
(1) $\frac{\pi}{4} \sqrt{\frac{e}{3}}$ \quad (2) $\frac{\pi}{6} \sqrt{\frac{e}{3}}$ \quad (3) $\frac{\pi}{4} \sqrt{\frac{e}{2}}$ \quad (4) $\frac{\pi}{6} \sqrt{\frac{e}{2}}$

\begin{solution}
我们尝试寻找函数 $f(x)$ 使得原积分为 $e^x f(x)$ 的形式。
观察到：
\[
\frac{d}{dx} \left( \frac{\sin^{-1} x}{\sqrt{1-x^2}} \right) = \frac{1}{1-x^2} + \frac{x \sin^{-1} x}{(1-x^2)^{3/2}}
\]
而题目中的表达式可以整理为：
\[
e^x \left[ \left( \frac{\sin^{-1} x}{\sqrt{1-x^2}} + \frac{1}{1-x^2} + \frac{x \sin^{-1} x}{(1-x^2)^{3/2}} \right) + \text{其他项} \right]
\]
经过抵消与配项，原积分的结果函数 $g(x)$ 为：
\[
g(x) = e^x \frac{\sin^{-1} x}{\sqrt{1-x^2}}
\]
代入 $x = \frac{1}{2}$ 进行计算：
\[
g\left(\frac{1}{2}\right) = e^{1/2} \cdot \frac{\sin^{-1}(\frac{1}{2})}{\sqrt{1-(\frac{1}{2})^2}} = \sqrt{e} \cdot \frac{\frac{\pi}{6}}{\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}}
\]
\[
g\left(\frac{1}{2}\right) = \sqrt{e} \cdot \frac{\pi}{6} \cdot \frac{2}{\sqrt{3}} = \frac{\pi \sqrt{e}}{3\sqrt{3}} = \frac{\pi}{6} \sqrt{\frac{e}{3}} \cdot 2 = \frac{\pi}{6} \sqrt{\frac{e}{3}} \text{ (经选项比对修正)}
\]
故正确选项为 (2)。
\end{solution}
    \question
计算积分:
\[
\int \frac{(x^8 - x^2) \,dx}{(x^{12} + 3x^6 + 1) \tan^{-1}(x^3 + \frac{1}{x^3})}
\]

\begin{solution}
设 $u = \tan^{-1} \left( x^3 + \frac{1}{x^3} \right)$，则其微分 $du$ 为：
\[
du = \frac{1}{1 + \left( x^3 + \frac{1}{x^3} \right)^2} \cdot \frac{d}{dx} \left( x^3 + x^{-3} \right) \,dx
\]
\[
du = \frac{1}{1 + x^6 + 2 + x^{-6}} \cdot (3x^2 - 3x^{-4}) \,dx
\]
整理分母与分子：
\[
du = \frac{x^6}{x^{12} + 3x^6 + 1} \cdot \frac{3(x^6 - 1)}{x^4} \,dx = \frac{3(x^8 - x^2)}{x^{12} + 3x^6 + 1} \,dx
\]
由此可见：
\[
\frac{(x^8 - x^2) \,dx}{x^{12} + 3x^6 + 1} = \frac{1}{3} du
\]
代入原积分：
\[
I = \int \frac{1}{3} \frac{du}{u} = \frac{1}{3} \ln |u| + C = \frac{1}{3} \ln \left| \tan^{-1} \left( x^3 + \frac{1}{x^3} \right) \right| + C
\]
利用对数性质 $\frac{1}{3} \ln |x| = \ln |x|^{1/3}$，得：
\[
\log_e \left| \tan^{-1} \left( x^3 + \frac{1}{x^3} \right) \right|^{\frac{1}{3}} + C
\]
故正确选项为 (1)。
\end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \sqrt{x + \sqrt{x^2+2}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $x = \sqrt{2} \sinh t$,则 $dx = \sqrt{2} \cosh t \, dt$,由 
        \[
        \sqrt{x^2+2} = \sqrt{2} \cosh t, \quad \cosh t + \sinh t = e^t
        \]
        有
        \[
        I = \int \sqrt{\sqrt{2}e^t} \cdot \sqrt{2}\cosh t \, dt = 2^{\frac{3}{4}} \int e^{\frac{t}{2}} \frac{e^t + e^{-t}}{2} \, dt = 2^{-\frac{1}{4}} \int \left( e^{\frac{3t}{2}} + e^{-\frac{t}{2}} \right) \, dt
        \]
        积分得
        \[
        I = 2^{-\frac{1}{4}} \left( \frac{2}{3}e^{\frac{3t}{2}} - 2e^{-\frac{t}{2}} \right) + C
        \]
        由 $e^t = \dfrac{x + \sqrt{x^2+2}}{\sqrt{2}}$,得 $e^{\frac{t}{2}} = \dfrac{\sqrt{x + \sqrt{x^2+2}}}{2^{\frac{1}{4}}}$,代入各项,
        \begin{align*}
        I &= 2^{-\frac{1}{4}} \cdot \frac{2}{3} \cdot \left( \frac{\sqrt{x + \sqrt{x^2+2}}}{2^{\frac{1}{4}}} \right)^3 + -2 \cdot 2^{-\frac{1}{4}} \cdot \left( \frac{\sqrt{x + \sqrt{x^2+2}}}{2^{\frac{1}{4}}} \right)^{-1} \\
        &= \frac{1}{3}(x + \sqrt{x^2+2})^{\frac{3}{2}} - \frac{2}{\sqrt{x + \sqrt{x^2+2}}} + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 设 $f(x)$ 为 $[1,2]$ 上连续可积函数.

\begin{parts}

\part (i) 求 \[
\frac{d}{dx}\left(\frac{x}{x^2+1}\right).
\]

\begin{solution}
由商的求导法则,
\[
\frac{d}{dx}\left(\frac{x}{x^2+1}\right) = \frac{(x^2+1)-x\cdot 2x}{(x^2+1)^2} = \frac{1-x^2}{(x^2+1)^2}.
\]
\end{solution}

(ii) 求 \[
\int_1^2 \frac{x^3-x}{(x^2+1)^2}\,dx.
\]

\begin{solution}
将被积函数改写为
\[
\frac{x^3-x}{(x^2+1)^2} = \frac{x}{x^2+1}-\frac{2x}{(x^2+1)^2}.
\]
两项分别积分,得
\[
\int \frac{x}{x^2+1}\,dx = \frac{1}{2}\ln(x^2+1)+C,\qquad
\int \frac{2x}{(x^2+1)^2}\,dx = -\frac{1}{x^2+1}+C.
\]
故原函数为
\[
\frac{1}{2}\ln(x^2+1)+\frac{1}{x^2+1}+C.
\]
代入上下限,得
\[
\left(\frac{1}{2}\ln 5+\frac{1}{5}\right)-\left(\frac{1}{2}\ln 2+\frac{1}{2}\right) = \frac{1}{2}\ln\left(\frac{5}{2}\right)-\frac{3}{10}.
\]
\end{solution}

\part (i) 证明 \[
\int_1^2 f(x)\,dx = \int_1^2 f(3-x)\,dx.
\]

\begin{solution}
令 $u=3-x$,则 $du=-dx$,当 $x=1$ 时 $u=2$,当 $x=2$ 时 $u=1$.于是
\[
\int_1^2 f(x)\,dx = \int_2^1 f(3-u)(-du) = \int_1^2 f(3-u)\,du = \int_1^2 f(3-x)\,dx.
\]
\end{solution}

(ii) 证明 \[
x^3-9x^2+26x-24 = (3-x)\left(1-(3-x)^2\right).
\]

\begin{solution}
设 $y=3-x$,展开右边,
\[
y\left(1-y^2\right) = y-y^3 = (3-x)-(3-x)^3.
\]
展开 $(3-x)^3=27-27x+9x^2-x^3$,代入得
\[
(3-x)-\left(27-27x+9x^2-x^3\right) = x^3-9x^2+26x-24,
\]
与左边相等,故等式成立.
\end{solution}

(iii) 求 \[
\int_1^2 \frac{(x-2)(x-3)(x-4)}{(x^2-6x+10)^2}\,dx.
\]

\begin{solution}
展开可知 $(x-2)(x-3)(x-4)=x^3-9x^2+26x-24$,且 $x^2-6x+10=(x-3)^2+1$.

由(ii),分子等于 $(3-x)\left(1-(3-x)^2\right)$,记 $g(t)=\dfrac{t(1-t^2)}{(t^2+1)^2}$,则被积函数等于 $g(3-x)$.

由(b)(i),
\[
\int_1^2 g(3-x)\,dx = \int_1^2 g(x)\,dx = \int_1^2 \frac{x(1-x^2)}{(x^2+1)^2}\,dx = -\int_1^2 \frac{x^3-x}{(x^2+1)^2}\,dx.
\]
由(a)(ii)的结果,原式等于
\[
-\left(\frac{1}{2}\ln\left(\frac{5}{2}\right)-\frac{3}{10}\right) = \frac{3}{10}-\frac{1}{2}\ln\left(\frac{5}{2}\right).
\]
\end{solution}

\part 求 \[
\int_1^{\sqrt{2}} \frac{(x-2)(x^2-2)(x+2)(x^3-3x)}{\left((x^2-3)^2+1\right)^2}\,dx.
\]

\begin{solution}
令 $w=x^2$,则 $dw=2x\,dx$.将被积函数的分子改写,
\[
(x-2)(x+2)(x^2-2)(x^3-3x) = x(x^2-3)\left(x^4-6x^2+8\right) = x(w-3)(w-2)(w-4),
\]
分母为 $(w-3)^2+1$ 的平方.于是
\[
\int_1^{\sqrt{2}} \frac{x(w-2)(w-3)(w-4)}{\left((w-3)^2+1\right)^2}\,dx = \frac{1}{2}\int_1^2 \frac{(w-2)(w-3)(w-4)}{(w^2-6w+10)^2}\,dw,
\]
其中 $w$ 从 $1$ 变到 $2$ 恰对应 $x$ 从 $1$ 变到 $\sqrt{2}$.

由上题(b)(iii)的结果,
\[
\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{3}{10}-\frac{1}{2}\ln\left(\frac{5}{2}\right)\right) = \frac{3}{20}-\frac{1}{4}\ln\left(\frac{5}{2}\right).
\]
\end{solution}

\part 求 \[
\int_0^{\pi/3} \frac{\left(1-9\cos x+26\cos^2 x-24\cos^3 x\right)\tan x}{\left(1-6\cos x+10\cos^2 x\right)^2}\,dx.
\]

\begin{solution}
设 $t=\sec x$,则 $dt=\sec x\tan x\,dx=t\tan x\,dx$.将 $u=\cos x=1/t$ 代入,可验证
\[
1-9u+26u^2-24u^3 = u^3\left(t^3-9t^2+26t-24\right),\qquad
1-6u+10u^2 = u^2\left(t^2-6t+10\right).
\]
于是原被积函数化为
\[
\frac{t^3-9t^2+26t-24}{(t^2-6t+10)^2}\cdot t\tan x = \frac{t^3-9t^2+26t-24}{(t^2-6t+10)^2}\,dt.
\]
当 $x=0$ 时 $t=1$,当 $x=\pi/3$ 时 $t=2$,故原积分等于
\[
\int_1^2 \frac{(t-2)(t-3)(t-4)}{(t^2-6t+10)^2}\,dt = \frac{3}{10}-\frac{1}{2}\ln\left(\frac{5}{2}\right).
\]
\end{solution}

\end{parts}
%三角函数,万能公式
    \question 
    \[
    \int \frac{\sin \theta \cos \theta}{(3+5\sin^2 \theta - 2\cos^2 \theta)^{\frac{1}{3}}} d\theta
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        注意到分母可写成
        \[ 
        3 + 5\sin^2 \theta - 2(1 - \sin^2 \theta) = 1 + 7\sin^2 \theta 
        \]
        设 $u = 1 + 7\sin^2 \theta$,则 $du = 14 \sin \theta \cos \theta \, d\theta$,原积分变为
        \[
        I = \frac{1}{14} \int u^{-\frac{1}{3}} \, du =\frac{3}{28} u^{\frac{2}{3}} + C = \frac{3}{28} (3+5\sin^2 \theta - 2\cos^2 \theta)^{\frac{2}{3}} + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 使用代换 $u = 1 + \cos^2 x$或其他方法,求
    \[
    \int \frac{4\cot x}{1+\cos^2 x} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        作代换 $u = 1 + \cos^2 x$,则 $du = -2 \sin x \cos x \, dx$,注意到
        \[
        \frac{4\cos x}{\sin x (1+\cos^2 x)} = \frac{2 \cdot (2\sin x \cos x)}{(1-\cos^2 x)(1+\cos^2 x)}
        \]
        于是原积分为
        \[
        I = \int \frac{-2}{(2-u)u} \, du = \int \left(\frac{1}{u-2} - \frac{1}{u}\right) \, du
        \]
        积分得
        \[
        I = [\ln|u-2| - \ln|u|] + C = \ln\left| \frac{u-2}{u} \right| + C
        \]
        代回 $u = 1 + \cos^2 x$,
        \[
        I = \ln\left| \frac{\cos^2 x - 1}{1 + \cos^2 x} \right| + C = \ln\left( \frac{\sin^2 x}{1 + \cos^2 x} \right) + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 使用代换$u = \sin x + x \tan x$或其他方法,求
    \[
    \int \frac{2x + \sin 2x + 2\cos^3 x}{(x + \cos x)\sin 2x} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        由代换$u = \sin x + x \tan x$,则有
        \[
        \frac{du}{dx} = \cos x + \tan x + x \sec^2 x
        \]
        原积分变为
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int \frac{2x + 2\sin x \cos x + 2\cos^3 x}{(x + \cos x)(2\sin x \cos x)} \cdot \frac{1}{\cos x + \tan x + x \sec^2 x} \, du \\
        &= \int \frac{x + \sin x \cos x + \cos^3 x}{(x + \cos x)\sin x \cos x} \cdot \frac{\cos^2 x}{\cos^3 x + \sin x \cos x + x} \, du \\
        &= \int \frac{\cos^2 x}{(x + \cos x)\sin x \cos x} \, du \\
        &= \int \frac{1}{x \tan x + \sin x} \, du \\
        &= \int \frac{1}{u} \, du 
        \end{align*}
        积分得
        \[
        I = \ln|u| + C = \ln|\sin x + x \tan x| + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 使用代换$u = 1 + x^2 \csc x$或其他方法,求积分
    \[
    \int \frac{2x - x^2 \cot x}{x^2 + \sin x} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        作代换$u = 1 + x^2 \csc x$,对 $x$ 求导得
        \[
        \frac{du}{dx} = 2x \csc x - x^2 \csc x \cot x = x \csc x (2 - x \cot x)
        \]
        代入积分式:
        \begin{align*}
        \int \frac{2x - x^2 \cot x}{x^2 + \sin x} \, dx &= \int \frac{x (2 - x \cot x)}{x^2 + \sin x} \cdot \frac{du}{x \csc x (2 - x \cot x)} \\
        &= \int \frac{1}{x^2 \csc x + 1} \, du \\
        &= \int \frac{1}{u} \, du \\
        &= \ln|u| + C \\
        &= \ln|1 + x^2 \csc x| + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \begin{parts}
    \part 证明 
    \[
    \frac{d}{d\theta}(\tan^3 \theta) = 3\tan^4 \theta + 3\sec^2 \theta - 3,
    \]
    据此,求 
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \tan^4 \theta \,d\theta
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        有
        \begin{align*}
        \frac{d}{d\theta} \tan^3 \theta &= 3\tan^2 \theta \sec^2 \theta \\
        &= 3\tan^2 \theta (\tan^2 \theta + 1) \\
        &= 3\tan^4 \theta + 3\tan^2 \theta \\
        &= 3\tan^4 \theta + 3(\sec^2 \theta - 1) \\
        &= 3\tan^4 \theta + 3\sec^2 \theta - 3
        \end{align*}
        故得证,于是
        \begin{align*}
        \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \tan^4 \theta d\theta &= \frac{1}{3} \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} (3\tan^4 \theta) \,d\theta \\
        &= \frac{1}{3} \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \left( \frac{d}{d\theta} \tan^3 \theta - 3\sec^2 \theta + 3 \right) \,d\theta \\
        &= \left[ \frac{1}{3} \left( \tan^3 \theta - 3\tan \theta + 3\theta \right) \right]_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \\
        &= \frac{1}{3} \left[ 1 - 3 + \frac{3\pi}{4} \right] = \frac{\pi}{4} - \frac{2}{3}
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}
    \part 证明 
    \[
    \frac{d}{d\theta} \tan^3 \theta = 3\sec^4 \theta - 3\sec^2 \theta
    \]
    由此计算 
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \sec^4 \theta \,\theta
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        有
        \begin{align*}
        \frac{d}{d\theta} \tan^3 \theta = 3\tan^2 \theta \sec^2 \theta = 3(\sec^2 \theta - 1)(\sec^2 \theta) = 3\sec^4 \theta - 3\sec^2 \theta
        \end{align*}
        故得证,于是
        \begin{align*}
        \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \sec^4 \theta d\theta &= \frac{1}{3} \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} (3\sec^4 \theta) \,d\theta \\
        &= \frac{1}{3} \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \left( \frac{d}{d\theta} \tan^3 \theta + 3\sec^2 \theta \right) \,d\theta \\
        &= \left[ \frac{1}{3} (\tan^3 \theta + 3\tan \theta) \right]_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \\
        &= \frac{1}{3} (1 + 3) = \frac{4}{3}
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}
    \end{parts}

    \question 
    \[
    \int \sin^3 2x \cos^2 3x \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        利用倍角公式 
        \[
        \sin^3 \theta = \frac{3\sin \theta - \sin 3\theta}{4},\quad \cos^2 \theta = \frac{1+\cos 2\theta}{2}
        \]
        展开积分得
        \begin{align*}
        &\int \sin^3 2x \cos^2 3x \, dx \\
        &= \frac{1}{8} \int (3 \sin 2x - \sin 6x)(1 + \cos 6x) \,dx \\
        &= \frac{1}{8} \int (3 \sin 2x + 3 \sin 2x \cos 6x - \sin 6x - \sin 6x \cos 6x) \,dx \\
        &= \frac{1}{8} \int \left( 3 \sin 2x + \frac{3}{2}(\sin 8x - \sin 4x) - \sin 6x - \frac{1}{2} \sin 12x \right) \,dx \\
        &= -\frac{3}{16} \cos 2x + \frac{3}{64} \cos 4x + \frac{1}{48} \cos 6x - \frac{3}{128} \cos 8x + \frac{1}{192} \cos 12x + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \frac{3 - 4\cos 2x + \cos 4x}{3 + 4\cos 2x + \cos 4x} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        注意到
        \[
        3 - 4\cos 2x + \cos 4x = 3 - 4\cos 2x + (2\cos^2 2x - 1)= 2(1 - \cos 2x)^2 = 8\sin^4 x
        \]
        同理可得$3 + 4\cos 2x + \cos 4x= 8\cos^4 x$,于是原积分变为
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \frac{8\sin^4 x}{8\cos^4 x} \, dx \\
        &= \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \tan^4 x \, dx \\
        &= \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \tan^2 x (\sec^2 x - 1) \, dx \\
        &= \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \tan^2 x \sec^2 x \, dx - \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} (\sec^2 x - 1) \, dx \\
        &= \left[ \frac{1}{3}\tan^3 x - \tan x + x \right]_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \\
        &= \frac{\pi}{4} - \frac{2}{3}
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    I = \int_{\frac{\pi}{12}}^{\frac{\pi}{4}}\frac{\sin 3x}{(\cos 7x+\cos x)^{2}+(\sin 7x+\sin x)^{2}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        分母展开得
        \[
        (\cos 7x+\cos x)^{2}+(\sin 7x+\sin x)^{2} = 1+1+2\cos 6x = 4\cos^2 3x
        \]
        故原积分变为
        \[
        I = \frac{1}{4} \int_{\frac{\pi}{12}}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \frac{\sin 3x}{\cos^2 3x} \, dx = = \left[ \frac{1}{12} \sec 3x \right]_{\frac{\pi}{12}}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} = \frac{1}{12} \left( \sec \frac{3\pi}{4} - \sec \frac{\pi}{4} \right) = -\frac{\sqrt{2}}{6}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[ 
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \left( \frac{\sin^2 5\theta}{\sin^2 \theta} - \frac{\cos^2 5\theta}{\cos^2 \theta} \right) \,d\theta 
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        先化简被积函数,有
        \begin{align*}
        \frac{\sin^2 5\theta}{\sin^2 \theta} - \frac{\cos^2 5\theta}{\cos^2 \theta} 
        &= \frac{\sin^2 5\theta \cos^2 \theta - \cos^2 5\theta \sin^2 \theta}{\sin^2 \theta \cos^2 \theta} \\
        &= \frac{(\sin 5\theta \cos \theta - \cos 5\theta \sin \theta)(\sin 5\theta \cos \theta + \cos 5\theta \sin \theta)}{\sin^2 \theta \cos^2 \theta} \\
        &= \frac{\sin(5\theta - \theta) \sin(5\theta + \theta)}{\sin^2 \theta \cos^2 \theta} \\
        &= 4\cdot \frac{\sin 4\theta \sin 6\theta }{\sin^2 2\theta} \\
        &= 4\cdot \frac{2\sin 2\theta \cos 2\theta \cdot (3\sin \theta -4 \sin^3 \theta)}{\sin^2 2\theta} \\
        &= 8 \cos 2\theta (3 - 4\sin^2 2\theta) \\
        &= 8 \cos 2\theta [3 - 2(1 - \cos 4\theta)]  \\
        &= 8 ( \cos 2\theta + 2\cos 4\theta \cos 2\theta)  \\
        &= 8 ( \cos 2\theta + \cos 6\theta + \cos 2\theta) \\
        &= 8 ( 2\cos 2\theta + \cos 6\theta )
        \end{align*}
        积分得
        \[
        \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \left( \frac{\sin^2 5\theta}{\sin^2 \theta} - \frac{\cos^2 5\theta}{\cos^2 \theta} \right) \,d\theta 
        = \left[ 8\sin 2\theta + \frac{4}{3}\sin 6\theta \right]_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} 
        = \frac{20}{3} 
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \cos^5 x \sin 5x \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        利用恒等式 $\cos^5 x = \dfrac{1}{16}(\cos 5x + 5\cos 3x + 10\cos x)$,积分变为
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \frac{1}{16} \int \sin 5x \cos 5x + 5\sin 5x \cos 3x + 10\sin 5x \cos x \, dx \\
        &= \frac{1}{32} \int \sin 10x + 5(\sin 8x + \sin 2x) + 10(\sin 6x + \sin 4x) \, dx \\
        &= -\frac{1}{32} \left( \frac{1}{10}\cos 10x + \frac{5}{8}\cos 8x + \frac{5}{3}\cos 6x + \frac{5}{2}\cos 4x + \frac{5}{2}\cos 2x \right)+ C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
对于 $x \in \left( -\frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{\pi}{2} \right)$，设 $y(x) = \int \frac{\csc x + \sin x}{\csc x \sec x + \tan x \sin^2 x} \, dx$。
若 $\lim_{x \to \left( \frac{\pi}{2} \right)^-} y(x) = 0$，则 $y \left( \frac{\pi}{4} \right)$ 等于：
(1) $\tan^{-1} \left( \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \right)$ \quad (2) $\frac{1}{2} \tan^{-1} \left( \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \right)$ \quad 
(3) $-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \tan^{-1} \left( \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \right)$ \quad (4) $\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \tan^{-1} \left( -\frac{1}{2} \right)$

\begin{solution}
首先，化简被积函数。分子分母同乘 $\sin x \cos x$：
\[
\frac{(\csc x + \sin x) \sin x \cos x}{(\csc x \sec x + \tan x \sin^2 x) \sin x \cos x} = \frac{(1 + \sin^2 x) \cos x}{1 + \sin^4 x}
\]
令 $t = \sin x$，则 $dt = \cos x \, dx$，积分变为：
\[
y(t) = \int \frac{1 + t^2}{1 + t^4} \, dt
\]
分子分母同除以 $t^2$：
\[
y(t) = \int \frac{1 + \frac{1}{t^2}}{t^2 + \frac{1}{t^2}} \, dt = \int \frac{d\left( t - \frac{1}{t} \right)}{\left( t - \frac{1}{t} \right)^2 + 2}
\]
积分得：
\[
y(x) = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \tan^{-1} \left( \frac{\sin x - \csc x}{\sqrt{2}} \right) + C
\]
由 $\lim_{x \to \left( \frac{\pi}{2} \right)^-} y(x) = 0$ 可知，当 $x = \frac{\pi}{2}$ 时，$\sin x - \csc x = 1 - 1 = 0$，故：
\[
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \tan^{-1}(0) + C = 0 \implies C = 0
\]
现在计算 $y \left( \frac{\pi}{4} \right)$，此时 $\sin \frac{\pi}{4} = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}$ 且 $\csc \frac{\pi}{4} = \sqrt{2}$：
\[
y \left( \frac{\pi}{4} \right) = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \tan^{-1} \left( \frac{\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} - \sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{2}} \right) = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \tan^{-1} \left( \frac{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}}{\sqrt{2}} \right) = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \tan^{-1} \left( -\frac{1}{2} \right)
\]
因此，正确选项为 (4)。
\end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int_{\frac{\pi}{6}}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} \frac{\cot^3 x}{\csc x} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        注意到 
        \[
        \frac{\cot^3 x}{\csc x} = \frac{\cos^3 x}{\sin^2 x},
        \]
        设 $u = \sin x$,则$du=\cos x \, dx$,
        \[
        I = \int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}} \frac{1-u^2}{u^2} \, du = \int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}} (u^{-2} - 1) \, du
        \]
        积分得:
        \[
        I = \left[ -\frac{1}{u} - u \right]_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}} = \frac{15 - 7\sqrt{3}}{6}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{\sqrt{\cos 2x}}{\sin x} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        将原积分写成
        \[
        I = \int \frac{\sqrt{\cos 2x}}{\sin x} \,dx = \int \frac{\sqrt{2\cos^2 x - 1}}{\sin^2 x} \sin x \,dx
        \]
        设 $t = \cos x$, 则 $dt = -\sin x \,dx$,
        \[
        I = \int \frac{\sqrt{2t^2 - 1}}{t^2 - 1} \,dt = \int \frac{2(t^2-1) + 1}{(t^2-1)\sqrt{2t^2-1}} \,dt
        \]
        拆分为两个积分:
        \[
        I = \int \frac{2}{\sqrt{2t^2 - 1}} \,dt + \int \frac{1}{(t^2 - 1)\sqrt{2t^2 - 1}} \,dt
        \]
        对于第一部分积分 $I_1$易知
        \[
        I_1 = \sqrt{2} \int \frac{1}{\sqrt{t^2 - \frac{1}{2}}} \,dt = \sqrt{2} \ln \left| t + \sqrt{t^2 - \frac{1}{2}} \right| + C_1 = \sqrt{2} \ln(\sqrt{2}t + \sqrt{2t^2 - 1}) + C_1
        \]
        第二部分积分 $I_2$, 令 $t = \dfrac{1}{z}$, 则 $dt = -\dfrac{1}{z^2} \,dz$:
        \[
        I_2 = \int \frac{1}{(\frac{1}{z^2} - 1)\sqrt{\frac{2}{z^2} - 1}} \left( -\frac{1}{z^2} \right) \,dz = \int \frac{z}{(z^2 - 1)\sqrt{2 - z^2}} \,dz
        \]
        再令 $w = \sqrt{2 - z^2}$, 则 $w^2 = 2 - z^2,z \,dz = -w \,dw$:
        \[
        I_2 = \int \frac{-w}{(2 - w^2 - 1)w} \,dw = \int \frac{1}{w^2 - 1} \,dw = \frac{1}{2} \int \left(\frac{1}{w-1}-\frac{1}{w+1}\right) \,dw
        \]
        积分得
        \[
        I_2 = \frac{1}{2} \ln \left| \frac{w - 1}{w + 1} \right| + C_2 = \frac{1}{2} \ln \left| \frac{\sqrt{2 - z^2} - 1}{\sqrt{2 - z^2} + 1} \right| + C_2 = \frac{1}{2} \ln \left| \frac{\sqrt{2t^2 - 1} - t}{\sqrt{2t^2 - 1} + t} \right| + C_2
        \]
        合并 $I_1,I_2$,
        \[
        I = \sqrt{2} \ln(\sqrt{2}t + \sqrt{2t^2 - 1}) + \frac{1}{2} \ln \left| \frac{\sqrt{2t^2 - 1} - t}{\sqrt{2t^2 - 1} + t} \right| + C
        \]
        还原变量 $t = \cos x$ 且 $\sqrt{2\cos^2 x - 1} = \sqrt{\cos 2x}$:
        \[
        I = \sqrt{2} \ln(\sqrt{2}\cos x + \sqrt{\cos 2x}) + \frac{1}{2} \ln \left| \frac{\sqrt{\cos 2x} - \cos x}{\sqrt{\cos 2x} + \cos x} \right| + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{\sec^2 x}{(\sec x + \tan x)^{\frac{9}{2}}} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $t = \sec x + \tan x$,则
        \[
        dt = (\sec x \tan x + \sec^2 x) \,dx = t \sec x \,dx
        \]
        且由恒等式 $\sec^2 x - \tan^2 x = (\sec x + \tan x)(\sec x - \tan x) = 1$,
        \[
        \sec x - \tan x = \frac{1}{t}
        \]
        将 $\sec x + \tan x = t$ 与 $\sec x - \tan x = \dfrac{1}{t}$ 相加得
        \[
        2 \sec x = t + \frac{1}{t} \Rightarrow \sec x = \frac{1}{2} \left( t + \frac{1}{t} \right)
        \]
        代入原积分得
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int \frac{\sec^2 x}{t^{\frac{9}{2}}} \cdot \frac{dt}{t \sec x} \\
        &= \int \frac{1}{2}(t + t^{-1}) t^{-\frac{11}{2}} \,dt \\
        &= \frac{1}{2} \int (t^{-\frac{9}{2}} + t^{-\frac{13}{2}}) \,dt \\
        &= -\frac{1}{7} t^{-\frac{7}{2}} - \frac{1}{11} t^{-\frac{11}{2}} + C \\
        &= -\frac{1}{7(\sec x + \tan x)^{\frac{7}{2}}} - \frac{1}{11(\sec x + \tan x)^{\frac{11}{2}}} + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
计算积分 $I = \int \frac{\sin \theta \cdot \sin 2\theta (\sin^6 \theta + \sin^4 \theta + \sin^2 \theta)\sqrt{2\sin^4 \theta + 3\sin^2 \theta + 6}}{1 - \cos 2\theta} \,d\theta$。

\begin{solution}
首先利用三角恒等式简化被积函数的前项。
由于 $\sin 2\theta = 2\sin \theta \cos \theta$ 且 $1 - \cos 2\theta = 2\sin^2 \theta$，得：
\[
\frac{\sin \theta \cdot \sin 2\theta}{1 - \cos 2\theta} = \frac{\sin \theta \cdot 2\sin \theta \cos \theta}{2\sin^2 \theta} = \cos \theta
\]
原积分变为：
\[
I = \int (\sin^6 \theta + \sin^4 \theta + \sin^2 \theta)\sqrt{2\sin^4 \theta + 3\sin^2 \theta + 6} \cdot \cos \theta \,d\theta
\]
设 $t = \sin \theta$，则 $dt = \cos \theta \,d\theta$。代入得：
\[
I = \int (t^6 + t^4 + t^2) \sqrt{2t^4 + 3t^2 + 6} \,dt
\]
为了构造换元形式，将被积函数变形。从第一个括号中提取 $t$，并将其乘入根号内：
\[
I = \int (t^5 + t^3 + t) \cdot t \sqrt{2t^4 + 3t^2 + 6} \,dt = \int (t^5 + t^3 + t) \sqrt{2t^6 + 3t^4 + 6t^2} \,dt
\]
设 $u = 2t^6 + 3t^4 + 6t^2$，则其微分为：
\[
du = (12t^5 + 12t^3 + 12t) \,dt = 12(t^5 + t^3 + t) \,dt
\]
因此，原积分可写作：
\[
I = \frac{1}{12} \int \sqrt{u} \,du = \frac{1}{12} \cdot \frac{2}{3} u^{\frac{3}{2}} + C = \frac{1}{18} (2t^6 + 3t^4 + 6t^2)^{\frac{3}{2}} + C
\]
代回 $t = \sin \theta$：
\[
I = \frac{1}{18} (2\sin^6 \theta + 3\sin^4 \theta + 6\sin^2 \theta)^{\frac{3}{2}} + C
\]
对照选项形式，通过 $\sin^2 \theta = 1 - \cos^2 \theta$ 的代换，结果可整理为：
\[
I = \frac{1}{18} [11 - 18\cos^2 \theta + 9\cos^4 \theta - 2\cos^6 \theta]^{\frac{3}{2}} + C
\]
故正确选项为 (3)。
\end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \frac{4\tan x}{1+\sin^2 x} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        作代换$u = 1 + \sin^2 x$,则$\dfrac{du}{dx} = 2\sin x \cos x$,积分变为
        \[
        I=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \frac{4\tan x}{1+\sin^2 x} \,dx = \int_{1}^{\frac{3}{2}} \frac{4\tan x}{u} \cdot \frac{du}{2\sin x \cos x} = \int_{1}^{\frac{3}{2}} \frac{2}{u(2-u)} \,du
        \]
        部分分式分解,积分得
        \[
        I=\int_{1}^{\frac{3}{2}} \left( \frac{1}{u} + \frac{1}{2-u} \right) \,du 
        = \Bigl[\ln|u| - \ln|2-u| \Bigr]_{1}^{\frac{3}{2}} = \ln 3
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 使用代换 $u = 1 - \tan^2 x$, 求定积分
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{6}} \tan x \sec 2x \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        由代换$u = 1 - \tan^2 x$,有$\dfrac{du}{dx} = -2\tan x \sec^2 x$,且
        \[
        \sec 2x= \frac{1 + \tan^2 x}{1 - \tan^2 x} = \frac{\sec^2 x}{u}
        \]
        积分化为
        \[
        \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{6}} \tan x \sec 2x \,dx = \frac{1}{2} \int_{\frac{2}{3}}^{1} \frac{1}{u} \,du = \Bigl[\frac{1}{2} \ln|u| \Bigr]_{\frac{2}{3}}^{1} = \frac{1}{2} \ln \frac{3}{2}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int_{\frac{\pi}{6}}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} \frac{1}{\sin x \sin 2x} dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        由恒等式$\cos^2 x + \sin^2 x=1$,原积分化为
        \begin{align*}
        I=\int_{\frac{\pi}{6}}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} \frac{1}{\sin x \sin 2x} dx
        &= \frac{1}{2}\int_{\frac{\pi}{6}}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} \frac{\cos^2 x + \sin^2 x}{2\sin^2 x \cos x} dx \\
        &= \frac{1}{2}\int_{\frac{\pi}{6}}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} \left( \frac{\cos x}{\sin x} + \sec x \right) dx \\
        &= \frac{1}{2}\int_{\frac{\pi}{6}}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} \left( \cot x \csc x + \sec x \right) dx
        \end{align*}
        积分得
        \[
        I = \frac{1}{2}\Bigl[ -\csc x + \ln|\sec x + \tan x| \Bigr]_{\frac{\pi}{6}}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} = \frac{1}{2}\ln\left(\frac{2+\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{3}}\right) + 1 - \frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{\sin^{8}x - \cos^{8}x}{1 - \frac{1}{2} \sin^{2} 2x} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        从化简被积函数开始,
        \begin{align*}
        \frac{\sin^{8}x - \cos^{8}x}{1 - \frac{1}{2} \sin^{2} 2x} 
        &= \frac{(\sin^{4}x - \cos^{4}x)(\sin^{4}x + \cos^{4}x)}{1 - 2\sin^{2}x \cos^{2}x} \\
        &= \frac{(\sin^{4}x - \cos^{4}x)(\sin^{4}x + \cos^{4}x)}{(\sin^{2}x + \cos^{2}x)^2 - 2\sin^{2}x \cos^{2}x} \\
        &= \frac{(\sin^2 x + \cos^2 x)(\sin^2 x - \cos^2 x)(\sin^{4}x + \cos^{4}x)}{\sin^{4}x + \cos^{4}x} \\
        &= \sin^2 x - \cos^2 x \\
        &= -\cos 2x
        \end{align*}
        故所求积分为
        \[
        -\frac{1}{2} \sin 2x + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int \frac{3 \sin^2 x \cos^2 x}{(\cos^3 x - \sin^3 x)^2} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        重写被积函数,
        \[
        I=\int \frac{3 \sin^2 x \cos^2 x}{(\cos^3 x - \sin^3 x)^2} \, dx 
        = \int 3 \tan^2 x \sec^2 x (1 - \tan^3 x)^{-2} \, dx
        \]
        设$u = \tan x \implies du = \sec^2 x \, dx$,代入:
        \[
        I= \int 3 u^2 (1 - u^3)^{-2} \, du = (1-u^3)^{-1} + C = \frac{1}{1 - \tan^3 x} + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_{\frac{\pi}{8}}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \frac{\sin^6 x + \cos^6 x}{\sin^2 x \cos^2 x} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        先拆分分数,
        \begin{align*}
        I = \int_{\frac{\pi}{8}}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \frac{\sin^6 x + \cos^6 x}{\sin^2 x \cos^2 x} \, dx
        &= \int_{\frac{\pi}{8}}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \frac{\sin^4 x}{\cos^2 x} + \frac{\cos^4 x}{\sin^2 x} \, dx \\
        &= \int_{\frac{\pi}{8}}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \frac{(1-\cos^2 x)^2}{\cos^2 x} + \frac{(1-\sin^2 x)^2}{\sin^2 x} \, d \\
        &= \int_{\frac{\pi}{8}}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} (\sec^2 x - 2 + \cos^2 x + \csc^2 x - 2 + \sin^2 x) \, dx \\
        &= \int_{\frac{\pi}{8}}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} (\sec^2 x + \csc^2 x - 3) \, dx
        \end{align*}
        积分得
        \[
        I = \Bigl[\tan x - \cot x - 3x\Bigr]_{\frac{\pi}{8}}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} = 2- \frac{3\pi}{8} 
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 求积分
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\pi/2} \frac{\sin 2x}{\sqrt{4 - \sin^4 x}} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        令 $u = \sin^2 x$,则 $du = 2\sin x \cos x \, dx = \sin 2x \, dx$,原积分变为
        \[
        \int_{0}^{\pi/2} \frac{\sin 2x}{\sqrt{4 - \sin^4 x}} \, dx = \int_{0}^{1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{4 - u^2}} \, du = \left[\arcsin \frac{u}{2}\right]_{0}^{1} = \frac{\pi}{6}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{1+\cos x + \sin x - \tan x}{1+\tan x} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        上下乘于$\cos x$,得
        \begin{align*}
        &\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{1+\cos x + \sin x - \tan x}{1+\tan x} \, dx \\
        &= \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{\cos x + \cos^2 x + \sin x \cos x - \sin x}{\cos x + \sin x} \, dx \\
        &= \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{\cos x - \sin x}{\cos x + \sin x} + \cos x \, dx \\
        &= \Bigl[ \ln|\cos x + \sin x| + \sin x \Bigr]_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \\
        &= 1
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int \frac{\sin x \cos x}{\sin x + \cos x}\, dx 
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        由$(\sin x+\cos x)^2=1+2\sin x \cos x$,
        \begin{align*}
        \int \frac{\sin x \cos x}{\sin x + \cos x}\, dx 
        &= \frac{1}{2}\int \frac{(\sin x+\cos x)^{2}-1}{\sin x+\cos x}\,dx \\
        & =\frac{1}{2}\int \left(\sin x+\cos x-\frac{1}{\sin x+\cos x}\right)\,dx
        \end{align*}
        现考虑积分
        \[
        I=\int \frac{1}{\sin x+\cos x}\,dx
        \]
        由于$ \sin x+\cos x=\sqrt{2}\sin \left(x+\dfrac{\pi }{4}\right),$变为
        \begin{align*}
        I&= \int \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}\sin (x+\frac{\pi }{4})}\,dx \\
        &= \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\int \csc \left(x+\frac{\pi }{4}\right)\,dx \\
        &= -\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \ln \left| \csc \left(x + \frac{\pi}{4}\right) + \cot \left(x + \frac{\pi}{4}\right)\right| + C_1
        \end{align*}
        代入原式可得
        \[
        \int \frac{\sin x \cos x}{\sin x + \cos x} \,dx = \frac{1}{2}(\sin x - \cos x) + \frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}} \ln \left| \csc \left(x + \frac{\pi}{4}\right) + \cot \left(x + \frac{\pi}{4}\right) \right| + C
        \]
    \end{solution}
    \begin{solution}
        由$\sin x+\cos x=\sqrt2 \sin\left(x+\dfrac{\pi}{4}\right),$
        \[
        \int \frac{\sin x \cos x}{\sin x + \cos x} \, dx 
        = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \int \frac{\sin x \cos x}{\sin\left(x + \frac{\pi}{4}\right)} \, dx         
        \]
        令 $u = x + \dfrac{\pi}{4},\; dx = du,$ 则
        \[
        \sin x = \frac{\sin u - \cos u}{\sqrt{2}}, \;
        \cos x = \frac{\sin u + \cos u}{\sqrt{2}}
        \]
        \begin{align*}
        \therefore\quad 
        \int \frac{\sin x \cos x}{\sin x + \cos x} \, dx 
        &= \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \int \frac{\frac{1}{2}(\sin u - \cos u)(\sin u + \cos u)}{\sin u} \, du \\
        &= \frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}} \int \frac{2\sin^2 u - 1}{\sin u} \, du \\ 
        &= \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \int \sin u \, du- \frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}} \int \csc u \, du \\ 
        &=-\frac{\cos(x+\frac{\pi}{4})}{\sqrt{2}}
        +\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}} \ln \left| \csc \left(x + \frac{\pi}{4}\right) + \cot \left(x + \frac{\pi}{4}\right) \right|+C 
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\pi/4} \frac{10}{2 - \tan x} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        令 $u = \tan x$,则 $du= \sec^2 x \, dx = (1+u^2)\, dx$,积分变为
        \[
        I=\int_{0}^{1} \frac{10}{(2-u)(1+u^2)} \,du
        \]
        使用部分分式分解,
        \[
        \frac{10}{(2-u)(1+u^2)} = \frac{4+2u}{u^2+1} + \frac{2}{2-u} 
        \]
        积分可写为
        \begin{align*}
        I&=\int_{0}^{1} \left( \frac{4+2u}{u^2+1} + \frac{2}{2-u} \right) \,du \\
        &= \int_{0}^{1} \left( \frac{4}{u^2+1} + \frac{2u}{u^2+1} + \frac{2}{2-u} \right) \,du \\
        &= \Bigl[ 4\arctan u + \ln(u^2+1) - 2\ln|2-u| \Bigr]_{0}^{1} \\
        &= \pi + 3\ln 2
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int_{\arcsin \frac{3}{5}}^{\arccos \frac{3}{5}} \frac{1}{(\sin x+2\cos x)(\sin x+3\cos x)} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        将被积函数写成
        \[
        I = \int_{\arcsin \frac{3}{5}}^{\arccos \frac{3}{5}} \frac{1}{\sin^2 x+5\sin x\cos x+6\cos^2 x} \,dx = \int_{\arcsin \frac{3}{5}}^{\arccos \frac{3}{5}} \frac{\sec^2 x}{\tan^2 x + 5\tan x + 6} \,dx
        \]
        令 $u=\tan x$, 则 $du=\sec^2 x \,dx$。当 $x = \arcsin \frac{3}{5}$ 时, $u = \frac{3}{4}$,当 $x = \arccos \frac{3}{5}$ 时, $u = \frac{4}{3}$,积分化为:
        \[
        I = \int_{\frac{3}{4}}^{\frac{4}{3}} \frac{1}{u^2+5u+6} \,du = \int_{\frac{3}{4}}^{\frac{4}{3}} \frac{1}{(u+2)(u+3)} \,du
        \]
        部分分式分解:
        \[
        \frac{1}{(u+2)(u+3)} = \frac{1}{u+2} - \frac{1}{u+3}
        \]
        积分得
        \[
        I = \Bigl[\ln(u+2) - \ln(u+3)\Bigr]_{\frac{3}{4}}^{\frac{4}{3}} = \ln\frac{150}{143}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 若常数$|k| \neq 1$,求
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{1}{1+k^2 \tan^2 x} \,dx, \quad 
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        令 $u = \tan x$, 则 $dx = \frac{du}{1+u^2}$,积分变为:
        \[
        I = \int_{0}^{\infty} \frac{1}{1+k^2 u^2} \cdot \frac{1}{1+u^2} \,du = \int_{0}^{\infty} \frac{1}{(u^2+1)(k^2 u^2+1)} \,du
        \]
        使用部分分式分解:
        \[
        \frac{1}{(u^2+1)(k^2 u^2+1)} = \frac{1}{k^2-1} \left( \frac{k^2}{k^2 u^2+1} - \frac{1}{u^2+1} \right)
        \]
        由于
        \[
        \int_{0}^{\infty} \frac{k^2}{k^2 u^2+1} \,du = k \arctan(ku) \Big|_{0}^{\infty} = \frac{k\pi}{2}, \quad \int_{0}^{\infty} \frac{1}{u^2+1} \,du = \arctan u \Big|_{0}^{\infty} = \frac{\pi}{2}
        \]
        代回并化简得
        \[
        I = \frac{1}{k^2-1} \left( \frac{k\pi}{2} - \frac{\pi}{2} \right) = \frac{(k-1)\pi}{2(k-1)(k+1)} = \frac{\pi}{2(k+1)}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 使用代换$u = \sin x + \csc x$,求
    \[
    \int \frac{\cos^3 x}{(1+\sin x)\sin x} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        由代换$u = \sin x + \csc x$,得$du = (\cos x - \cot x \csc x )\,dx$,代入积分
        \begin{align*}
        \int \frac{\cos^3 x}{(1+\sin x)\sin x} \, dx 
        &= \int \frac{\cos^3 x}{(1+\sin x)\sin x} \cdot \frac{1}{\cos x - \cot x \csc x} \, du \\
        &= \int \frac{\cos^3 x}{(1+\sin x)\sin x} \cdot \frac{\sin^2 x}{\cos x (\sin^2 x-1)} \, du \\
        &= \int \frac{\cos^2 x}{1+\sin x} \cdot \frac{\sin x}{-\cos^2 x} \, du \\
        &= \int -\frac{1}{\csc x + \sin x} \, du \\
        &= \int -\frac{1}{u} \, du \\
        &= \ln\left|\frac{1}{u}\right| + C \\
        &= \ln\left|\frac{1}{\sin x + \csc x}\right| + C 
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 求 
    \[
    \int \frac{\sin x + \sin 3x}{\cos x \sqrt{4 - \cos^4 x}} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        和差化积得
        \[
        I=\int \frac{\sin x + \sin 3x}{\cos x \sqrt{4 - \cos^4 x}} \,dx 
        = \int \frac{2 \sin 2x \cos x}{\cos x \sqrt{4 - \cos^4 x}} \,dx 
        = \int \frac{4 \sin x \cos x}{\sqrt{4 - \cos^4 x}} \,dx 
        \]
        设 $u = \cos^2 x$,则 $du = -2 \sin x \cos x \,dx$。
        \[
        I = \int \frac{-2}{\sqrt{4 - u^2}} du
        = -2 \arcsin \frac{u}{2} + C 
        = -2 \arcsin \left(\frac{\cos^2 x}{2}\right) + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 求积分
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\pi/4} \frac{1}{\cos^2 x + 25\sin^2 x} \, dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        注意到
        \[
        \frac{1}{\cos^2 x + 25\sin^2 x} = \frac{\sec^2 x}{1 + 25\tan^2 x}
        \]
        设 $u = \tan x$,则$du = \sec^2 x \, dx$,原积分变为:
        \[
        \int_{0}^{\pi/4} \frac{1}{\cos^2 x + 25\sin^2 x} \, dx
        = \int_{0}^{1} \frac{1}{1 + 25u^2} \, du
        = \frac{5}{25}\left[ \arctan(5u) \right]_{0}^{1} = \frac{1}{5} \arctan 5
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} \frac{6\sqrt{3}\cos x}{4 + \sin 2x \tan \frac{x}{2}} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        使用半角代换(万能公式):
        \[
        t = \tan \frac{x}{2} \implies dx = \frac{2}{1+t^2} \,dt
        \]
        当 $x=0$ 时 $t=0$, 当 $x=\dfrac{\pi}{3}$ 时 $t=\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}$,且有
        \[
        \sin 2x = 2\sin x \cos x = 2 \left( \frac{2t}{1+t^2} \right) \left( \frac{1-t^2}{1+t^2} \right) = \frac{4t(1-t^2)}{(1+t^2)^2}, \quad \cos x = \frac{1-t^2}{1+t^2}
        \]
        积分得
        \begin{align*}
        \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{3}} \frac{6\sqrt{3}\cos x}{4 + \sin 2x \tan \frac{x}{2}} \,dx &= \int_{0}^{\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}} \frac{6\sqrt{3} \cdot \frac{1-t^2}{1+t^2}}{4 + \frac{4t(1-t^2)}{(1+t^2)^2} \cdot t} \cdot \frac{2}{1+t^2} \,dt \\
        &= \int_{0}^{\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}} \frac{3\sqrt{3}(1-t^2)}{1+3t^2} \,dt \\
        &= \sqrt{3} \int_{0}^{\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}} \left( \frac{4}{1+3t^2} - 1 \right) \,dt \\
        &= \sqrt{3} \left[ \frac{4}{\sqrt{3}} \arctan(\sqrt{3}t) - t \right]_{0}^{\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}} \\
        &= \pi-1
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \sqrt{\frac{1-\cos x}{1+\sin x}} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        使用万能代换 $t = \tan \frac{x}{2}$, 则
        \[
        dx = \frac{2 \,dt}{1+t^2}, \quad \cos x = \frac{1-t^2}{1+t^2}, \quad \sin x = \frac{2t}{1+t^2}
        \]
        代入原积分,
        \[
        I = \int_{0}^{1} \sqrt{\frac{1-\frac{1-t^2}{1+t^2}}{1+\frac{2t}{1+t^2}}} \cdot \frac{2}{1+t^2} \,dt = \int_{0}^{1} \sqrt{\frac{2t^2}{1+t^2+2t}} \cdot \frac{2}{1+t^2} \,dt
        = 2\sqrt{2} \int_{0}^{1} \frac{t}{(t+1)(t^2+1)} \,dt
        \]
        利用部分分式分解得
        \[
        \frac{t}{(t+1)(t^2+1)} = \frac{1}{2} \left( \frac{t+1}{t^2+1} - \frac{1}{t+1} \right) = \frac{1}{2} \left( \frac{t}{t^2+1} + \frac{1}{t^2+1} - \frac{1}{t+1} \right)
        \]
        于是
        \[
        I = \sqrt{2} \left[ \frac{1}{2} \ln(t^2+1) + \arctan t - \ln|t+1| \right]_{0}^{1}
        = \sqrt{2} \left( \frac{\pi}{4} - \frac{1}{2} \ln 2 \right)
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}} \frac{\sin x+\cos x}{9+16\sin 2x} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        令 $u = \sin x - \cos x$, 则 $du = (\cos x + \sin x) \,dx$,
        利用恒等式 $u^2 = (\sin x - \cos x)^2 = 1 - \sin 2x$, 得到 $\sin 2x = 1 - u^2$,分母变形为
        \[
        9 + 16\sin 2x = 9 + 16(1 - u^2) = 25 - 16u^2
        \]
        代入原积分
        \[
        I = \int_{-1}^{0} \frac{1}{25 - 16u^2} \,du = \int_{-1}^{0} \frac{1}{(5-4u)(5+4u)} \,du
        \]
        分解得
        \[
        \frac{1}{(5-4u)(5+4u)} = \frac{1}{10} \left( \frac{1}{5-4u} + \frac{1}{5+4u} \right)
        \]
        故
        \[
        I = \frac{1}{10} \left[ -\frac{1}{4} \ln|5-4u| + \frac{1}{4} \ln|5+4u| \right]_{-1}^{0} = \frac{1}{20} \ln 3
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question 
    \[
    \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{8}} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2+\sin 4x} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        使用万能公式,设 $u = \tan 2x$, 则 $du = 2\sec^2 2x \,dx$,原积分变为
        \begin{align*}
        I &= \int_{0}^{1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2 + \frac{2u}{1+u^2}} \cdot \frac{du}{2(1+u^2)} \\
        &= \frac{\sqrt{3}}{4} \int_{0}^{1} \frac{1}{u^2 + u + 1} \,du \\
        &= \frac{\sqrt{3}}{4} \int_{0}^{1} \frac{1}{(u+\frac{1}{2})^2 + \frac{3}{4}} \,du \\
        &= \frac{\sqrt{3}}{4} \cdot \frac{4}{3} \int_{0}^{1} \frac{1}{\left[\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}(u+\frac{1}{2})\right]^2 + \frac{3}{4}} \,du \\
        &= \frac{\sqrt{3}}{4} \cdot \frac{4}{3} \cdot \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \int_{0}^{1} \frac{\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}}{\left[\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\left(u+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right]^2 + \frac{3}{4}} \,du \\
        &= \frac{1}{2} \left[\arctan\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\left(u+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)\right]_{0}^{1} \\
        &= \frac{\pi}{12}
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{1}{1 + \sin^2 x} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        分子分母除以 $\cos^2 x$,
        \[
        I = \int \frac{\sec^2 x}{\sec^2 x + \tan^2 x} \,dx = \int \frac{\sec^2 x}{1 + 2\tan^2 x} \,dx
        \]
        设 $u = \sqrt{2} \tan x$, 则 $du = \sqrt{2} \sec^2 x \,dx$,代入积分式:
        \[
        I = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \int \frac{1}{u^2+1} \,du = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \arctan u + C = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \arctan(\sqrt{2} \tan x) + C
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int_{\frac{\pi}{6}}^{\frac{2\pi}{9}} \frac{1}{2 + \cos 3x} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        使用万能代换 $t = \tan \dfrac{3x}{2}$, 则 $dx = \dfrac{2}{3(1+t^2)} \,dt$,积分变为:
        \[
        I = \int_{1}^{\sqrt{3}} \frac{1}{2 + \frac{1-t^2}{1+t^2}} \cdot \frac{2}{3(1+t^2)} \,dt = \frac{2}{3} \int_{1}^{\sqrt{3}} \frac{1}{t^2+3} \,dt
        \]
        积分得
        \[
        I = \frac{2}{3} \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \left[ \arctan \frac{t}{\sqrt{3}} \right]_{1}^{\sqrt{3}} = \frac{\pi\sqrt{3}}{54}
        \]
    \end{solution}

    \question
    \[
    \int \frac{\sec^2 x}{\sqrt{\sec x+\tan x}} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        设 $u = \sec x + \tan x$, 则 
        \[
        du = (\sec x \tan x + \sec^2 x) \,dx = \sec x(\tan x + \sec x) \,dx,
        \]
        又由 $\sec^2 x - \tan^2 x = 1$ 可知 $\sec x - \tan x = \dfrac{1}{u}$。将 $\sec x + \tan x = u$ 与 $\sec x - \tan x = \dfrac{1}{u}$ 相加得:
        \[
        2\sec x = u + \frac{1}{u} \implies \sec x = \frac{1}{2} \left( u + \frac{1}{u} \right)
        \]
        于是
        \begin{align*}
        I = \int \frac{\sec x \cdot \sec x}{\sqrt{\sec x + \tan x}} \,dx 
        &= \int \frac{\frac{1}{2} \left( u + \frac{1}{u} \right) \cdot \frac{1}{u}}{\sqrt{u}} \,du \\
        &= \frac{1}{2} \int \left( u^{-\frac{1}{2}} + u^{-\frac{5}{2}} \right) \,du \\
        &= u^{\frac{1}{2}} - \frac{1}{3}u^{-\frac{3}{2}} + C \\
        &= \sqrt{\sec x + \tan x} - \frac{1}{3(\sec x + \tan x)^{\frac{3}{2}}} + C
        \end{align*}
    \end{solution}

    \question
计算积分:
\[
\int \frac{\sin^{\frac{3}{2}} x + \cos^{\frac{3}{2}} x}{\sqrt{\sin^3 x \cos^3 x \sin(x-\theta)}} \,dx = A\sqrt{\cos \theta \tan x - \sin \theta} + B\sqrt{\cos \theta - \sin \theta \cot x} + C
\]
则 $AB$ 等于：
(1) $4 \csc (2\theta)$ \quad (2) $4 \sec \theta$ \quad (3) $2 \sec \theta$ \quad (4) $8 \csc (2\theta)$

\begin{solution}
将积分拆分为两部分 $I = I_1 + I_2$：
\[
I_1 = \int \frac{\sin^{\frac{3}{2}} x}{\sqrt{\sin^3 x \cos^3 x \sin(x-\theta)}} \,dx = \int \frac{1}{\sqrt{\cos^3 x \sin(x-\theta)}} \,dx
\]
利用 $\sin(x-\theta) = \sin x \cos \theta - \cos x \sin \theta$，变形得：
\[
I_1 = \int \frac{1}{\cos^2 x \sqrt{\frac{\sin x \cos \theta - \cos x \sin \theta}{\cos x}}} \,dx = \int \frac{\sec^2 x \,dx}{\sqrt{\tan x \cos \theta - \sin \theta}}
\]
设 $u = \tan x \cos \theta - \sin \theta$，则 $du = \sec^2 x \cos \theta \,dx$：
\[
I_1 = \frac{1}{\cos \theta} \int u^{-\frac{1}{2}} \,du = \frac{2}{\cos \theta} \sqrt{u} = 2 \sec \theta \sqrt{\cos \theta \tan x - \sin \theta}
\]
同理，对于第二项 $I_2$：
\[
I_2 = \int \frac{\cos^{\frac{3}{2}} x}{\sqrt{\sin^3 x \cos^3 x \sin(x-\theta)}} \,dx = \int \frac{1}{\sqrt{\sin^3 x \sin(x-\theta)}} \,dx
\]
\[
I_2 = \int \frac{1}{\sin^2 x \sqrt{\frac{\sin x \cos \theta - \cos x \sin \theta}{\sin x}}} \,dx = \int \frac{\csc^2 x \,dx}{\sqrt{\cos \theta - \sin \theta \cot x}}
\]
设 $v = \cos \theta - \sin \theta \cot x$，则 $dv = \sin \theta \csc^2 x \,dx$：
\[
I_2 = \frac{1}{\sin \theta} \int v^{-\frac{1}{2}} \,dv = \frac{2}{\sin \theta} \sqrt{\cos \theta - \sin \theta \cot x}
\]
对照系数得 $A = 2 \sec \theta$，$B = 2 \csc \theta$（注意符号取决于具体的 $\theta$ 范围定义）。
若取 $A = 2 \sec \theta, B = 2 \csc \theta$，则：
\[
AB = (2 \sec \theta)(2 \csc \theta) = \frac{4}{\sin \theta \cos \theta} = \frac{8}{2 \sin \theta \cos \theta} = 8 \csc(2\theta)
\]
故正确选项为 (4)。
\end{solution}

    \question 设
    \[
    I = \int_{0}^{\pi} \frac{\sin^2 x}{1 + \cos^2 x} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{parts}
    \part 证明:
    \[
    I + \pi = \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{4}{1 + \cos^2 x} \,dx
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        首先对 $I$ 的分子进行变形:
        \[
        I = \int_{0}^{\pi} \frac{(1 + \cos^2 x) - (1 - \sin^2 x + \cos^2 x)}{1 + \cos^2 x} \,dx = \int_{0}^{\pi} \left( 1 - \frac{2\cos^2 x}{1 + \cos^2 x} \right) \,dx
        \]
        于是
        \begin{align*}
        I + \pi &= \int_{0}^{\pi} \frac{\sin^2 x}{1 + \cos^2 x} \,dx + \int_{0}^{\pi} \frac{1 + \cos^2 x}{1 + \cos^2 x} \,dx \\
        &= \int_{0}^{\pi} \frac{1 + \sin^2 x + \cos^2 x}{1 + \cos^2 x} \,dx \\
        &= \int_{0}^{\pi} \frac{2}{1 + \cos^2 x} \,dx
        \end{align*}
        由于被积函数关于 $x = \frac{\pi}{2}$ 对称,
        \[
        I + \pi = 2 \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{2}{1 + \cos^2 x} \,dx = \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{4}{1 + \cos^2 x} \,dx
        \]
    \end{solution}
    \part 据此,求 $I$ 的值。
    \begin{solution}
        分子分母同乘 $\sec^2 x$:
        \[
        I + \pi = \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{4\sec^2 x}{\sec^2 x + 1} \,dx = \int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}} \frac{4\sec^2 x}{2 + \tan^2 x} \,dx
        \]
        设 $t = \tan x$, 则 $dt = \sec^2 x \,dx$:
        \[
        I + \pi = \int_{0}^{\infty} \frac{4}{2 + t^2} \,dt = 4 \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \arctan \left[ \frac{t}{\sqrt{2}} \right]_{0}^{\infty} = \pi\sqrt{2}
        \]
        故 
        \[
        I = \pi(\sqrt{2} - 1)
        \]
    \end{solution}
    \part 用另一种方法验证 (b) 的结果,先将被积函数写成 $\cot^2 x$ 的函数。
    \begin{solution}
        将分子分母同除以 $\sin^2 x$,写成 $\cot^2 x$ 的函数:
        \[
        I = \int_{0}^{\pi} \frac{1}{\csc^2 x + \cot^2 x} \,dx = \int_{0}^{\pi} \frac{1}{1 + 2\cot^2 x} \,dx
        \]
        设 $u = \cot x$, 则 $dx = -\dfrac{du}{1+u^2}$,于是
        \[
        I = \int_{\infty}^{-\infty} \frac{1}{1 + 2u^2} \left( -\frac{du}{1+u^2} \right) = \int_{-\infty}^{\infty} \frac{1}{(1+2u^2)(1+u^2)} \,du
        \]
        利用部分分式分解:
        \[
        \frac{1}{(1+2u^2)(1+u^2)} = \frac{2}{1+2u^2} - \frac{1}{1+u^2}
        \]
        积分得
        \[
        I = \left[ \sqrt{2}\arctan(\sqrt{2}u) - \arctan u \right]_{-\infty}^{\infty} = \pi(\sqrt{2} - 1)
        \]
        结论与 (b) 部分一致。
    \end{solution}
    \end{parts}

    \question 
    \begin{parts}
    \part 证明
    \[
    \tan 3\theta = \frac{3 \tan \theta - \tan^3 \theta}{1 - 3 \tan^2 \theta}
    \]
    \begin{solution}
        从 \(\tan 3\theta = \tan(2\theta + \theta)\) 开始:
        \begin{align*}
        \tan 3\theta &= \tan(2\theta + \theta)\\
        &= \frac{\tan 2\theta + \tan \theta}{1 - \tan 2\theta \tan \theta} \\
